MODEL     : qwen/qwen3.8-2.4t-a95b
METRIC    : area_delay_product
RESULT    : PASS  |  best cost: 6.342e+05 area_delay_product  (eval 17)
STEPS: 60  |  duration: 9901.2s

[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Optimize the execution-stage ALU of the CV32E40P (RI5CY) RISC-V core: a module
named cv32e40p_alu implementing the full RV32IM ALU operation set plus the PULP
(XPULP) extensions: SIMD vector modes, bit-manipulation, bit-counting,
shuffle/pack, clip, and a multi-cycle serial integer divider behind a ready
handshake.

A complete, known-correct implementation is provided in your workspace as
starting_point.sv: the upstream CV32E40P ALU flattened into one self-contained
file (top module cv32e40p_alu plus its popcount, find-first-one and serial-divider
submodules; package constants inlined). Start from it and optimize. Any
restructuring is legal as long as the I/O behaviour stays cycle-identical to it:
re-associating or factoring arithmetic, sharing or splitting datapaths, cheaper
operator implementations, a different divider architecture with the same cycle
count, and so on.

Ports:
  input         clk                  // rising-edge clock (used only by div/rem)
  input         rst_n                // active-low reset
  input         enable_i             // operation strobe (relevant for div/rem)
  input  [6:0]  operator_i           // ALU opcode, encodings below
  input  [31:0] operand_a_i
  input  [31:0] operand_b_i
  input  [31:0] operand_c_i
  input  [1:0]  vector_mode_i        // 2'b00: one 32-bit lane, 2'b10: two 16-bit
                                     // lanes, 2'b11: four 8-bit lanes
  input  [4:0]  bmask_a_i            // bit-mask length-1 / rounding control
  input  [4:0]  bmask_b_i            // bit-mask offset / round-shift amount
  input  [1:0]  imm_vec_ext_i        // lane selector for EXT/EXTS/INS
  input         is_clpx_i            // complex-arithmetic modifier
  input         is_subrot_i          // sub-rotate modifier (SUB only)
  input  [1:0]  clpx_shift_i         // post-shift amount when is_clpx_i
  output [31:0] result_o
  output        comparison_result_o  // scalar flag of comparison ops
  output        ready_o              // result handshake (see protocol)
  input         ex_ready_i           // downstream ready

Contract (checked by the testbench, an equivalence checker): your design runs
side by side with the upstream ALU on the same stimulus. At every falling clock
edge after reset, ready_o must equal the upstream ALU's ready_o; whenever the
upstream ALU is ready, result_o must equal its result, and comparison_result_o
as well for the 14 comparison opcodes (for every other opcode that output is a
don't-care). Concretely:
  * Every opcode except DIV/DIVU/REM/REMU is combinational in the starting point:
    ready_o is high at the first falling edge after the inputs are applied, and
    high in the idle state. It must stay that way: a design that registers these
    results and answers a cycle later fails, however correct the values.
  * DIV/DIVU/REM/REMU take several cycles: ready_o must fall and rise in exactly
    the cycles of the starting point's divider. Its latency is data dependent
    (it depends on the leading bits of the divisor operand_a_i and on the
    signedness of the operation; see cv32e40p_alu_div and the div_shift
    computation in starting_point.sv). A faster or slower divider fails.
  * A div/rem computation must not disturb later back-to-back operations.

How the testbench drives the design:
  * rst_n is held low for the first 5 clock cycles, then stays high. A synchronous
    or asynchronous active-low reset are both acceptable.
  * For each operation, all inputs are driven at a falling clock edge with
    enable_i=1 and ex_ready_i=1 and are held stable; ready_o is then sampled at
    each following falling edge (cap 64 cycles) and compared with the upstream
    ALU's. At the first falling edge where the upstream ALU is ready, result_o
    (and comparison_result_o where applicable) are compared. After sampling,
    enable_i is deasserted for one cycle before the next operation is applied.
  * Stimulus: the 20,000 vectors of vectors.dat (directed corner cases and
    seeded random operations, 15% div/rem), a sweep of the divider over every
    divisor and dividend bit length, and 1,000,000 random operations generated in
    the testbench from a fixed seed, all within the driven envelope below.
    Correctness is judged solely by this testbench.
  * tb.sv and vectors.dat sit in your workspace for reference. The second half of
    tb.sv is a renamed copy of starting_point.sv (the reference model); there is
    nothing new to learn from reading it.

Opcode encodings (operator_i, 7-bit). Only these 57 values are ever driven; the
behavior for any other encoding is a don't-care:

  Adder family:      ADD  7'b0011000  SUB  7'b0011001  ADDU 7'b0011010  SUBU 7'b0011011
                     ADDR 7'b0011100  SUBR 7'b0011101  ADDUR 7'b0011110 SUBUR 7'b0011111
  Logic:             AND 7'b0010101   OR 7'b0101110    XOR 7'b0101111
  Shifts:            SRA 7'b0100100   SRL 7'b0100101   ROR 7'b0100110   SLL 7'b0100111
  Bit-manipulation:  BEXT 7'b0101000  BEXTU 7'b0101001 BINS 7'b0101010
                     BCLR 7'b0101011  BSET 7'b0101100  BREV 7'b1001001
  Bit-count:         FF1 7'b0110110   FL1 7'b0110111   CNT 7'b0110100   CLB 7'b0110101
  Extract/insert:    EXTS 7'b0111110  EXT 7'b0111111   INS 7'b0101101
  Vector compare:    LTS 7'b0000000   LTU 7'b0000001   LES 7'b0000100   LEU 7'b0000101
                     GTS 7'b0001000   GTU 7'b0001001   GES 7'b0001010   GEU 7'b0001011
                     EQ  7'b0001100   NE  7'b0001101
  Scalar set-less:   SLTS 7'b0000010  SLTU 7'b0000011  SLETS 7'b0000110 SLETU 7'b0000111
  Min/max/abs/clip:  MIN 7'b0010000   MINU 7'b0010001  MAX 7'b0010010   MAXU 7'b0010011
                     ABS 7'b0010100   CLIP 7'b0010110  CLIPU 7'b0010111
  Shuffle/pack:      SHUF 7'b0111010  SHUF2 7'b0111011 PCKLO 7'b0111000 PCKHI 7'b0111001
  Serial divider:    DIVU 7'b0110000  DIV 7'b0110001   REMU 7'b0110010  REM 7'b0110011

Vector (SIMD) lanes: vector_mode_i partitions the 32-bit datapath into one 32-bit,
two 16-bit, or four 8-bit independent lanes (2'b01 is never driven). "Per lane"
below means the operation is applied independently in each lane of a and b.

Semantics by group ("s" = two's-complement signed, "u" = unsigned):

* Adder family (per lane): SUB/SUBU/SUBR/SUBUR compute a-b, the others a+b.
  The lane results are then post-processed on the concatenated 32-bit value:
    1. A rounding constant is added (plain 32-bit add, carries may cross lanes):
       for ADDR/SUBR/ADDUR/SUBUR the constant is bmask>>1 where
       bmask = (~(32'hFFFFFFFE << bmask_a_i)) << bmask_b_i
       (a run of bmask_a_i+1 ones starting at bit bmask_b_i); for
       ADD/SUB/ADDU/SUBU the constant is 0.
    2. The sum is right-shifted per lane: arithmetically for ADD/SUB/ADDR/SUBR,
       logically for the U variants. The per-lane shift amount is normally
       bmask_b_i (truncated to the lane's amount width: 5/4/3 bits for
       32/16/8-bit lanes); when is_clpx_i=1 it is clpx_shift_i instead.
  With bmask_a_i=bmask_b_i=0 (the common case) this is a plain per-lane add/sub.
  is_subrot_i=1 (only ever driven with SUB, two 16-bit lanes): before the
  subtraction the operands' halves are cross-swapped: the effective operands are
  {b[15:0], a[31:16]} minus {a[15:0], b[31:16]} (per-lane 16-bit subtraction) —
  i.e. result high half = b_lo - a_lo, low half = a_hi - b_hi.

* Logic: AND = a&b, OR = a|b, XOR = a^b (lane-independent).

* Shifts (per lane): SLL logical left, SRL logical right, SRA arithmetic right.
  Per-lane shift amounts come from the corresponding lane of operand_b_i,
  truncated to 5/4/3 bits for 32/16/8-bit lanes (e.g. in 32-bit mode: b[4:0];
  in 16-bit mode: lane 0 uses b[3:0], lane 1 uses b[19:16]).
  ROR in 32-bit mode rotates a right by b[4:0]. (In 16/8-bit vector modes ROR
  is driven but degenerates to a per-lane logical right shift, matching the
  shared shifter datapath.)

* Vector comparisons (per lane): LTS/LTU a<b, LES/LEU a<=b, GTS/GTU a>b,
  GES/GEU a>=b (S = signed, U = unsigned), EQ a==b, NE a!=b.
  result_o: each lane is filled with all-ones if the lane comparison is true,
  else all-zeros. comparison_result_o = the result of the most-significant lane
  (the whole-word result in 32-bit mode).
  Scalar set-less (32-bit only): SLTS/SLTU (a<b), SLETS/SLETU (a<=b);
  result_o = {31'b0, flag} and comparison_result_o = flag.
  comparison_result_o is only checked for these 14 comparison opcodes; for every
  other opcode it is a don't-care.

* MIN/MINU/MAX/MAXU (per lane): minimum / maximum of a and b (signed for
  MIN/MAX, unsigned for MINU/MAXU).

* ABS (per lane; operand_b_i is always 0 for ABS): |a| (two's-complement
  negation of negative lanes; the most negative value wraps to itself).
  With is_clpx_i=1 (driven only in 16-bit mode): complex conjugate —
  result = {-a[31:16], a[15:0]}.

* CLIP/CLIPU (32-bit only, any operand_b_i value; in practice b = 2^k - 1):
  Let lo = ~b (i.e. -(b+1)) and hi = b, as signed values.
    CLIP:  if a <s lo or a == lo -> lo; else if a >s hi -> hi (signed min of
           a and b); else a. I.e. clamp(a, ~b, b) for b >= 0.
    CLIPU: if a <=s 0 -> 0; else signed-min(a, b).

* Extract/insert (16/8-bit vector modes only; operand_c_i is always 0 for
  EXT/EXTS): sel = imm_vec_ext_i (2 bits in 8-bit mode, only bit 0 in 16-bit
  mode).
    EXT:  zero-extend lane sel of a to 32 bits.
    EXTS: sign-extend lane sel of a to 32 bits.
    INS:  result = operand_c_i with lane sel replaced by the low lane of a
          (a[7:0] in 8-bit mode, a[15:0] in 16-bit mode).

* Shuffle/pack (16/8-bit vector modes only). Notation: x.B[i] = byte i of x,
  x.H[i] = 16-bit half i of x.
    SHUF  8-bit:  result.B[k] = a.B[ b.B[k][1:0] ]           for k=3..0
    SHUF  16-bit: result.H[k] = a.H[ b.H[k][0] ]             for k=1..0
    SHUF2 8-bit:  result.B[k] = (b.B[k][2] ? a : c).B[ b.B[k][1:0] ]
    SHUF2 16-bit: result.H[k] = (b.H[k][1] ? a : c).H[ b.H[k][0] ]
    PCKLO 16-bit: {a[15:0],  b[15:0]}
    PCKHI 16-bit: {a[31:16], b[31:16]}
    PCKLO 8-bit:  {c[31:16], a[7:0], b[7:0]}
    PCKHI 8-bit:  {a[7:0], b[7:0], c[15:0]}

* Bit-manipulation (32-bit mode only). Define
  bmask = (~(32'hFFFFFFFE << bmask_a_i)) << bmask_b_i (a run of bmask_a_i+1
  ones starting at bit bmask_b_i, truncated at bit 31).
    BCLR:  a & ~bmask
    BSET:  a | bmask
    BEXTU: (a >> b[4:0]) & bmask                      (logical shift)
    BEXT:  t = a >>> b[4:0] (arithmetic shift);
           result = (t & bmask) | ({32{t[bmask_a_i]}} & ~bmask)
           (extract then sign-extend from bit bmask_a_i)
    BINS:  ((a << b[4:0]) & bmask) | (c & ~bmask)
    BREV:  t = a << b[4:0]; then reverse t in groups selected by bmask_a_i[1:0]:
           0: single bits (full 32-bit bit-reversal of t)
           1: 2-bit groups: result[2j+1:2j] = t[31-2j : 30-2j] for j=0..15
           2: 3-bit groups: result[3j+2:3j] = t[31-3j : 29-3j] for j=0..9,
              result[31:30] = 0
           3: same as 0
  (In practice b[4:0] equals bmask_b_i for BEXT/BEXTU/BINS, but arbitrary
  combinations are driven and must match the formulas above.)

* Bit-count (32-bit mode only, result on 6 bits zero-extended to 32):
    CNT: number of set bits in a
    FF1: index of the least-significant set bit of a, or 32 if a==0
    FL1: index of the most-significant set bit of a, or 32 if a==0
    CLB: count of leading bits of a equal to the sign bit a[31], minus one
         (i.e. redundant sign bits); special case a==0 -> 0. Examples:
         a=32'hFFFFFFFF -> 31, a=32'h00000001 -> 30, a=32'h40000000 -> 0.

* Serial divider (32-bit mode only). NOTE the operand roles: the DIVIDEND is
  operand_b_i and the DIVISOR is operand_a_i. RISC-V M-extension semantics:
    DIVU: b /u a;  a==0 -> 32'hFFFFFFFF
    DIV:  b /s a (quotient rounds toward zero);  a==0 -> 32'hFFFFFFFF;
          b==0x80000000, a==0xFFFFFFFF -> 0x80000000
    REMU: b %u a;  a==0 -> b
    REM:  b %s a (sign follows the dividend);  a==0 -> b;
          b==0x80000000, a==0xFFFFFFFF -> 0
  These four ops go through the serial divider and take several cycles; the
  cycle count must be exactly the starting point's (see the contract above).

Driven input envelope (anything outside is a don't-care, testbench never drives
it): only the 57 opcodes above; vector_mode_i in {00,10,11} and restricted to
32-bit mode for logic*, bit-manip, bit-count, scalar set-less, clip and div/rem
(*logic ops are lane-independent anyway); bmask_a_i/bmask_b_i are nonzero only
for the adder family and bit-manip ops; imm_vec_ext_i nonzero only for
EXT/EXTS/INS; is_clpx_i only for ABS (16-bit mode) and the adder family;
is_subrot_i only for SUB (16-bit mode); clpx_shift_i nonzero only when
is_clpx_i=1; ABS sees operand_b_i==0 only; EXT/EXTS see operand_c_i==0 only.

Everything outside the driven envelope is a don't-care and is never exercised.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

module cv32e40p_alu
(
    input logic               clk,
    input logic               rst_n,
    input logic               enable_i,
    input logic [ 6:0] operator_i,
    input logic        [31:0] operand_a_i,
    input logic        [31:0] operand_b_i,
    input logic        [31:0] operand_c_i,

    input logic [1:0] vector_mode_i,
    input logic [4:0] bmask_a_i,
    input logic [4:0] bmask_b_i,
    input logic [1:0] imm_vec_ext_i,

    input logic       is_clpx_i,
    input logic       is_subrot_i,
    input logic [1:0] clpx_shift_i,

    output logic [31:0] result_o,
    output logic        comparison_result_o,

    output logic ready_o,
    input  logic ex_ready_i
);

  // ---- constants inlined from cv32e40p_pkg.sv (mechanical flatten) ----

  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SUB = 7'b0011001;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ADDU = 7'b0011010;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SUBU = 7'b0011011;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ADDR = 7'b0011100;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SUBR = 7'b0011101;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ADDUR = 7'b0011110;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SUBUR = 7'b0011111;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_XOR = 7'b0101111;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_OR = 7'b0101110;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_AND = 7'b0010101;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SRA = 7'b0100100;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SRL = 7'b0100101;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ROR = 7'b0100110;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SLL = 7'b0100111;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BEXT = 7'b0101000;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BEXTU = 7'b0101001;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BINS = 7'b0101010;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BCLR = 7'b0101011;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BSET = 7'b0101100;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BREV = 7'b1001001;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_FF1 = 7'b0110110;

  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLB = 7'b0110101;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EXTS = 7'b0111110;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EXT = 7'b0111111;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_LTS = 7'b0000000;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_LTU = 7'b0000001;

[Step 3/60]
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_LTU = 7'b0000001;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_LES = 7'b0000100;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_LEU = 7'b0000101;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_GTS = 7'b0001000;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_GTU = 7'b0001001;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_GES = 7'b0001010;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_GEU = 7'b0001011;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EQ = 7'b0001100;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_NE = 7'b0001101;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SLTS = 7'b0000010;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SLTU = 7'b0000011;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SLETS = 7'b0000110;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SLETU = 7'b0000111;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ABS = 7'b0010100;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLIP = 7'b0010110;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLIPU = 7'b0010111;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_INS = 7'b0101101;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MIN = 7'b0010000;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MINU = 7'b0010001;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MAX = 7'b0010010;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MAXU = 7'b0010011;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_DIVU = 7'b0110000;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_DIV = 7'b0110001;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_REMU = 7'b0110010;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_REM = 7'b0110011;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SHUF = 7'b0111010;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SHUF2 = 7'b0111011;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_PCKLO = 7'b0111000;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_PCKHI = 7'b0111001;
  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE32 = 2'b00;
  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE16 = 2'b10;
  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE8 = 2'b11;

  logic [31:0] operand_a_rev;
  logic [31:0] operand_a_neg;
  logic [31:0] operand_a_neg_rev;

  assign operand_a_neg = ~operand_a_i;

  // bit reverse operand_a for left shifts and bit counting
  generate
    genvar k;
    for (k = 0; k < 32; k++) begin : gen_operand_a_rev
      assign operand_a_rev[k] = operand_a_i[31-k];
    end
  endgenerate

  // bit reverse operand_a_neg for left shifts and bit counting
  generate
    genvar m;
    for (m = 0; m < 32; m++) begin : gen_operand_a_neg_rev
      assign operand_a_neg_rev[m] = operand_a_neg[31-m];
    end
  endgenerate

  logic [31:0] operand_b_neg;

  assign operand_b_neg = ~operand_b_i;


  logic [ 5:0] div_shift;
  logic        div_valid;
  logic [31:0] bmask;

  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  //   ____            _   _ _   _                      _      _       _     _            //
  //  |  _ \ __ _ _ __| |_(_) |_(_) ___  _ __   ___  __| |    / \   __| | __| | ___ _ __  //
  //  | |_) / _` | '__| __| | __| |/ _ \| '_ \ / _ \/ _` |   / _ \ / _` |/ _` |/ _ \ '__| //
  //  |  __/ (_| | |  | |_| | |_| | (_) | | | |  __/ (_| |  / ___ \ (_| | (_| |  __/ |    //
  //  |_|   \__,_|_|   \__|_|\__|_|\___/|_| |_|\___|\__,_| /_/   \_\__,_|\__,_|\___|_|    //
  //                                                                                      //
  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

  logic        adder_op_b_negate;
  logic [31:0] adder_op_a, adder_op_b;
  logic [35:0] adder_in_a, adder_in_b;
  logic [31:0] adder_result;
  logic [36:0] adder_result_expanded;


  assign adder_op_b_negate = (operator_i == ALU_SUB) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR) ||
                             (operator_i == ALU_SUBU) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBUR) || is_subrot_i;

  // prepare operand a
  assign adder_op_a = (operator_i == ALU_ABS) ? operand_a_neg : (is_subrot_i ? {
    operand_b_i[15:0], operand_a_i[31:16]
  } : operand_a_i);

  // prepare operand b
  assign adder_op_b = adder_op_b_negate ? (is_subrot_i ? ~{
    operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[31:16]
  } : operand_b_neg) : operand_b_i;

  // prepare carry
  always_comb begin
    adder_in_a[0]     = 1'b1;
    adder_in_a[8:1]   = adder_op_a[7:0];
    adder_in_a[9]     = 1'b1;
    adder_in_a[17:10] = adder_op_a[15:8];
    adder_in_a[18]    = 1'b1;
    adder_in_a[26:19] = adder_op_a[23:16];
    adder_in_a[27]    = 1'b1;
    adder_in_a[35:28] = adder_op_a[31:24];

    adder_in_b[0]     = 1'b0;
    adder_in_b[8:1]   = adder_op_b[7:0];
    adder_in_b[9]     = 1'b0;
    adder_in_b[17:10] = adder_op_b[15:8];
    adder_in_b[18]    = 1'b0;
    adder_in_b[26:19] = adder_op_b[23:16];
    adder_in_b[27]    = 1'b0;
    adder_in_b[35:28] = adder_op_b[31:24];

    if (adder_op_b_negate || (operator_i == ALU_ABS || operator_i == ALU_CLIP)) begin
      // special case for subtractions and absolute number calculations
      adder_in_b[0] = 1'b1;

      case (vector_mode_i)
        VEC_MODE16: begin
          adder_in_b[18] = 1'b1;
        end

        VEC_MODE8: begin
          adder_in_b[9]  = 1'b1;
          adder_in_b[18] = 1'b1;
          adder_in_b[27] = 1'b1;
        end
      endcase

    end else begin
      // take care of partitioning the adder for the addition case
      case (vector_mode_i)
        VEC_MODE16: begin
          adder_in_a[18] = 1'b0;
        end

        VEC_MODE8: begin
          adder_in_a[9]  = 1'b0;
          adder_in_a[18] = 1'b0;
          adder_in_a[27] = 1'b0;
        end
      endcase
    end
  end

  // actual adder
  assign adder_result_expanded = $signed(adder_in_a) + $signed(adder_in_b);
  assign adder_result = {
    adder_result_expanded[35:28],
    adder_result_expanded[26:19],
    adder_result_expanded[17:10],
    adder_result_expanded[8:1]
  };


  // normalization stage
  logic [31:0] adder_round_value;
  logic [31:0] adder_round_result;

  assign adder_round_value  = ((operator_i == ALU_ADDR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR) ||
                               (operator_i == ALU_ADDUR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBUR)) ?
                                {
    1'b0, bmask[31:1]
  } : '0;
  assign adder_round_result = adder_result + adder_round_value;


  ////////////////////////////////////////
  //  ____  _   _ ___ _____ _____       //
  // / ___|| | | |_ _|  ___|_   _|      //
  // \___ \| |_| || || |_    | |        //
  //  ___) |  _  || ||  _|   | |        //
  // |____/|_| |_|___|_|     |_|        //
  //                                    //
  ////////////////////////////////////////

  logic        shift_left;  // should we shift left
  logic        shift_use_round;
  logic        shift_arithmetic;

  logic [31:0] shift_amt_left;  // amount of shift, if to the left
  logic [31:0] shift_amt;  // amount of shift, to the right
  logic [31:0] shift_amt_int;  // amount of shift, used for the actual shifters
  logic [31:0] shift_amt_norm;  // amount of shift, used for normalization
  logic [31:0] shift_op_a;  // input of the shifter
  logic [31:0] shift_result;
  logic [31:0] shift_right_result;
  logic [31:0] shift_left_result;
  logic [15:0] clpx_shift_ex;

  // shifter is also used for preparing operand for division
  assign shift_amt = div_valid ? div_shift : operand_b_i;

  // by reversing the bits of the input, we also have to reverse the order of shift amounts
  always_comb begin
    case (vector_mode_i)
      VEC_MODE16: begin
        shift_amt_left[15:0]  = shift_amt[31:16];
        shift_amt_left[31:16] = shift_amt[15:0];
      end

      VEC_MODE8: begin
        shift_amt_left[7:0]   = shift_amt[31:24];
        shift_amt_left[15:8]  = shift_amt[23:16];
        shift_amt_left[23:16] = shift_amt[15:8];
        shift_amt_left[31:24] = shift_amt[7:0];
      end

      default: // VEC_MODE32
      begin
        shift_amt_left[31:0] = shift_amt[31:0];
      end
    endcase
  end

  // ALU_FL1 and ALU_CBL are used for the bit counting ops later
  assign shift_left = (operator_i == ALU_SLL) || (operator_i == ALU_BINS) ||
                      (operator_i == ALU_FL1) || (operator_i == ALU_CLB)  ||
                      (operator_i == ALU_DIV) || (operator_i == ALU_DIVU) ||
                      (operator_i == ALU_REM) || (operator_i == ALU_REMU) ||
                      (operator_i == ALU_BREV);

  assign shift_use_round = (operator_i == ALU_ADD)   || (operator_i == ALU_SUB)   ||
                           (operator_i == ALU_ADDR)  || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR)  ||
                           (operator_i == ALU_ADDU)  || (operator_i == ALU_SUBU)  ||
                           (operator_i == ALU_ADDUR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBUR);

  assign shift_arithmetic = (operator_i == ALU_SRA)  || (operator_i == ALU_BEXT) ||
                            (operator_i == ALU_ADD)  || (operator_i == ALU_SUB)  ||
                            (operator_i == ALU_ADDR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR);

  // choose the bit reversed or the normal input for shift operand a
  assign shift_op_a    = shift_left ? operand_a_rev :
                          (shift_use_round ? adder_round_result : operand_a_i);
  assign shift_amt_int = shift_use_round ? shift_amt_norm :
                          (shift_left ? shift_amt_left : shift_amt);

  assign shift_amt_norm = is_clpx_i ? {clpx_shift_ex, clpx_shift_ex} : {4{3'b000, bmask_b_i}};

  assign clpx_shift_ex = $unsigned(clpx_shift_i);

  // right shifts, we let the synthesizer optimize this
  logic [63:0] shift_op_a_32;

  assign shift_op_a_32 = (operator_i == ALU_ROR) ? {
        shift_op_a, shift_op_a
      } : $signed(
          {{32{shift_arithmetic & shift_op_a[31]}}, shift_op_a}
      );

  always_comb begin
    case (vector_mode_i)
      VEC_MODE16: begin
        shift_right_result[31:16] = $signed(
            {shift_arithmetic & shift_op_a[31], shift_op_a[31:16]}
        ) >>> shift_amt_int[19:16];
        shift_right_result[15:0] = $signed(
            {shift_arithmetic & shift_op_a[15], shift_op_a[15:0]}
        ) >>> shift_amt_int[3:0];
      end

      VEC_MODE8: begin
        shift_right_result[31:24] = $signed(
            {shift_arithmetic & shift_op_a[31], shift_op_a[31:24]}
        ) >>> shift_amt_int[26:24];
        shift_right_result[23:16] = $signed(
            {shift_arithmetic & shift_op_a[23], shift_op_a[23:16]}
        ) >>> shift_amt_int[18:16];
        shift_right_result[15:8] = $signed(
            {shift_arithmetic & shift_op_a[15], shift_op_a[15:8]}
        ) >>> shift_amt_int[10:8];
        shift_right_result[7:0] = $signed(
            {shift_arithmetic & shift_op_a[7], shift_op_a[7:0]}
        ) >>> shift_amt_int[2:0];
      end

      default: // VEC_MODE32
      begin
        shift_right_result = shift_op_a_32 >> shift_amt_int[4:0];
      end
    endcase
    ;  // case (vec_mode_i)
  end

  // bit reverse the shift_right_result for left shifts
  genvar j;
  generate
    for (j = 0; j < 32; j++) begin : gen_shift_left_result
      assign shift_left_result[j] = shift_right_result[31-j];
    end
  endgenerate

  assign shift_result = shift_left ? shift_left_result : shift_right_result;


  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  //   ____ ___  __  __ ____   _    ____  ___ ____   ___  _   _   //
  //  / ___/ _ \|  \/  |  _ \ / \  |  _ \|_ _/ ___| / _ \| \ | |  //
  // | |  | | | | |\/| | |_) / _ \ | |_) || |\___ \| | | |  \| |  //
  // | |__| |_| | |  | |  __/ ___ \|  _ < | | ___) | |_| | |\  |  //

... (showing lines 98-397 of 1452; use offset/limit for more)

[ASSISTANT]
  -> read_file(filename='starting_point.sv', offset=397, limit=350)

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 4/60]
  // | |__| |_| | |  | |  __/ ___ \|  _ < | | ___) | |_| | |\  |  //
  //  \____\___/|_|  |_|_| /_/   \_\_| \_\___|____/ \___/|_| \_|  //
  //                                                              //
  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

  logic [ 3:0] is_equal;
  logic [ 3:0] is_greater;  // handles both signed and unsigned forms

  // 8-bit vector comparisons, basic building blocks
  logic [ 3:0] cmp_signed;
  logic [ 3:0] is_equal_vec;
  logic [ 3:0] is_greater_vec;
  logic [31:0] operand_b_eq;
  logic        is_equal_clip;


  //second == comparator for CLIP instructions
  always_comb begin
    operand_b_eq = operand_b_neg;
    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) operand_b_eq = '0;
    else operand_b_eq = operand_b_neg;
  end
  assign is_equal_clip = operand_a_i == operand_b_eq;

  always_comb begin
    cmp_signed = 4'b0;

    unique case (operator_i)
      ALU_GTS,
      ALU_GES,
      ALU_LTS,
      ALU_LES,
      ALU_SLTS,
      ALU_SLETS,
      ALU_MIN,
      ALU_MAX,
      ALU_ABS,
      ALU_CLIP,
      ALU_CLIPU: begin
        case (vector_mode_i)
          VEC_MODE8:  cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1111;
          VEC_MODE16: cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1010;
          default:    cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1000;
        endcase
      end

      default: ;
    endcase
  end

  // generate vector equal and greater than signals, cmp_signed decides if the
  // comparison is done signed or unsigned
  genvar i;
  generate
    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : gen_is_vec
      assign is_equal_vec[i] = (operand_a_i[8*i+7:8*i] == operand_b_i[8*i+7:i*8]);
      assign is_greater_vec[i] = $signed(
          {operand_a_i[8*i+7] & cmp_signed[i], operand_a_i[8*i+7:8*i]}
      ) > $signed(
          {operand_b_i[8*i+7] & cmp_signed[i], operand_b_i[8*i+7:i*8]}
      );
    end
  endgenerate

  // generate the real equal and greater than signals that take the vector
  // mode into account
  always_comb begin
    // 32-bit mode
    is_equal[3:0] = {4{is_equal_vec[3] & is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[1] & is_equal_vec[0]}};
    is_greater[3:0] = {4{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & (is_greater_vec[2]
                                            | (is_equal_vec[2] & (is_greater_vec[1]
                                             | (is_equal_vec[1] & (is_greater_vec[0]))))))}};

    case (vector_mode_i)
      VEC_MODE16: begin
        is_equal[1:0]   = {2{is_equal_vec[0] & is_equal_vec[1]}};
        is_equal[3:2]   = {2{is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[3]}};
        is_greater[1:0] = {2{is_greater_vec[1] | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])}};
        is_greater[3:2] = {2{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & is_greater_vec[2])}};
      end

      VEC_MODE8: begin
        is_equal[3:0]   = is_equal_vec[3:0];
        is_greater[3:0] = is_greater_vec[3:0];
      end

      default: ;  // see default assignment
    endcase
  end

  // generate comparison result
  logic [3:0] cmp_result;

  always_comb begin
    cmp_result = is_equal;
    unique case (operator_i)
      ALU_EQ:                                 cmp_result = is_equal;
      ALU_NE:                                 cmp_result = ~is_equal;
      ALU_GTS, ALU_GTU:                       cmp_result = is_greater;
      ALU_GES, ALU_GEU:                       cmp_result = is_greater | is_equal;
      ALU_LTS, ALU_SLTS, ALU_LTU, ALU_SLTU:   cmp_result = ~(is_greater | is_equal);
      ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU, ALU_LES, ALU_LEU: cmp_result = ~is_greater;
      default:                                ;
    endcase
  end

  assign comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];


  // min/max/abs handling

  logic [ 3:0] sel_minmax;
  logic        do_min;
  logic [31:0] minmax_b;

  assign minmax_b = (operator_i == ALU_ABS) ? adder_result : operand_b_i;

  assign result_minmax[7:0] = (sel_minmax[0] == 1'b1) ? operand_a_i[7:0] : minmax_b[7:0];

  // Clip
  //////////////////////////////////////////////////

  always_comb begin
    clip_result = result_minmax;
    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) begin

      end else begin

  logic [3:0][1:0] shuffle_byte_sel;  // select byte in register: 31:24, 23:16, 15:8, 7:0

      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),
      .OpB_DI      (shift_left_result),
      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),
      .OpBIsZero_SI((cnt_result == 0)),

      .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),
      .OpCode_SI (operator_i[1:0]),

      .Res_DO(result_div),

      // Hand-Shake
      .InVld_SI (div_valid),
      .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),
      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)
  );

  ////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  //   ____                 _ _     __  __              //
  //  |  _ \ ___  ___ _   _| | |_  |  \/  |_   ___  __  //
  //  | |_) / _ \/ __| | | | | __| | |\/| | | | \ \/ /  //
  //  |  _ <  __/\__ \ |_| | | |_  | |  | | |_| |>  <   //
  //  |_| \_\___||___/\__,_|_|\__| |_|  |_|\__,_/_/\_\  //
  //                                                    //
  ////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

  always_comb begin
    result_o = '0;

    unique case (operator_i)
      // Standard Operations
      ALU_AND: result_o = operand_a_i & operand_b_i;
      ALU_OR:  result_o = operand_a_i | operand_b_i;
      ALU_XOR: result_o = operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i;

      // Shift Operations
      ALU_ADD, ALU_ADDR, ALU_ADDU, ALU_ADDUR,
      ALU_SUB, ALU_SUBR, ALU_SUBU, ALU_SUBUR,
      ALU_SLL,
      ALU_SRL, ALU_SRA,
      ALU_ROR:
      result_o = shift_result;

      // bit manipulation instructions
      ALU_BINS, ALU_BEXT, ALU_BEXTU: result_o = bextins_result;

      ALU_BCLR: result_o = bclr_result;
      ALU_BSET: result_o = bset_result;

      // Bit reverse instruction
      ALU_BREV: result_o = reverse_result;

      // pack and shuffle operations
      ALU_SHUF, ALU_SHUF2, ALU_PCKLO, ALU_PCKHI, ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS, ALU_INS: result_o = pack_result;

      // Min/Max/Ins
      ALU_MIN, ALU_MINU, ALU_MAX, ALU_MAXU: result_o = result_minmax;

      //Abs/Cplxconj , ABS is used to do 0 - A for Cplxconj
      ALU_ABS: result_o = is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]} : result_minmax;

      ALU_CLIP, ALU_CLIPU: result_o = clip_result;

      // Comparison Operations
      ALU_EQ, ALU_NE, ALU_GTU, ALU_GEU, ALU_LTU, ALU_LEU, ALU_GTS, ALU_GES, ALU_LTS, ALU_LES: begin
        result_o[31:24] = {8{cmp_result[3]}};
        result_o[23:16] = {8{cmp_result[2]}};
        result_o[15:8]  = {8{cmp_result[1]}};
        result_o[7:0]   = {8{cmp_result[0]}};
      end
      // Non-vector comparisons
      ALU_SLTS, ALU_SLTU, ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU: result_o = {31'b0, comparison_result_o};

      ALU_FF1, ALU_FL1, ALU_CLB, ALU_CNT: result_o = {26'h0, bitop_result[5:0]};

      // Division Unit Commands
      ALU_DIV, ALU_DIVU, ALU_REM, ALU_REMU: result_o = result_div;

      default: ;  // default case to suppress unique warning
    endcase
  end

  assign ready_o = div_ready;

endmodule

  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;
  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;

  genvar l, m, n, p;

    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1

    end
  endgenerate

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// Engineer:       Andreas Traber - atraber@student.ethz.ch                   //
//                                                                            //
// Additional contributions by:                                               //
//                 Davide Schiavone - pschiavo@iis.ee.ethz.ch                 //
//                                                                            //
// Design Name:    cv32e40p_ff_one                                               //
// Project Name:   RI5CY                                                      //
// Language:       SystemVerilog                                              //
//                                                                            //
// Description:    Find First One                                             //
//                                                                            //
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

module cv32e40p_ff_one #(
    parameter LEN = 32
) (
    input logic [LEN-1:0] in_i,

    output logic [$clog2(LEN)-1:0] first_one_o,
    output logic                   no_ones_o
);

  localparam NUM_LEVELS = $clog2(LEN);

  logic [          LEN-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_lut;
  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0]                 sel_nodes;
  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_nodes;


  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  // generate tree structure
  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

  generate
    genvar j;
    for (j = 0; j < LEN; j++) begin : gen_index_lut
      assign index_lut[j] = $unsigned(j);
    end
  endgenerate

  generate
    genvar k;
    genvar l;
    genvar level;

    assign sel_nodes[2**NUM_LEVELS-1] = 1'b0;

    for (level = 0; level < NUM_LEVELS; level++) begin : gen_tree
      //------------------------------------------------------------
      if (level < NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_non_root_level
        for (l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : gen_node
          assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+l]   = sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] | sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];
          assign index_nodes[2**level-1+l] = (sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] == 1'b1) ?
                                           index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] : index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];
        end
      end
      //------------------------------------------------------------
      if (level == NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_root_level
        for (k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : gen_node
          // if two successive indices are still in the vector...
          if (k * 2 < LEN - 1) begin : gen_two
            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k] = in_i[k*2] | in_i[k*2+1];
            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = (in_i[k*2] == 1'b1) ? index_lut[k*2] : index_lut[k*2+1];
          end
          // if only the first index is still in the vector...
          if (k * 2 == LEN - 1) begin : gen_one
            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = in_i[k*2];
            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = index_lut[k*2];
          end
          // if index is out of range
          if (k * 2 > LEN - 1) begin : gen_out_of_range
            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = 1'b0;
            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = '0;
          end
        end
      end
      //------------------------------------------------------------
    end
  endgenerate

  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  // connect output
  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

  assign first_one_o = index_nodes[0];
  assign no_ones_o   = ~sel_nodes[0];

endmodule

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// File       : Simple Serial Divider
// Ver        : 1.0
// Date       : 15.03.2016
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
// Description: this is a simple serial divider for signed integers (int32).
//
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
// Authors    : Michael Schaffner (schaffner@iis.ee.ethz.ch)
//              Andreas Traber    (atraber@iis.ee.ethz.ch)
//
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

module cv32e40p_alu_div #(
    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,
    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6
) (
    input  logic                   Clk_CI,
    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,
    // input IF
    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,
    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,
    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,
    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,
    //
    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,  // gate this to 0 in case of unsigned ops
    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,  // 0: udiv, 2: urem, 1: div, 3: rem
    // handshake
    input  logic                   InVld_SI,
    // output IF
    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,
    output logic                   OutVld_SO,
    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO
);

  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  // signal declarations
  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;

  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;
  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;
  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;

  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;

  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;
  logic CntZero_S;

  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;

  enum logic [1:0] {
    IDLE,
    DIVIDE,
    FINISH
  }
      State_SN, State_SP;


  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  // datapath
  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));

  // muxes
  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;

  // attention: logical shift in case of negative operand B!
  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};

  genvar index;
  generate
    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping
      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];
    end
  endgenerate

  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;

  // invert if necessary
  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;

  // main comparator
  assign ABComp_S    = ((AReg_DP == BReg_DP) | ((AReg_DP > BReg_DP) ^ CompInv_SP)) & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);

  // main adder
  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;
  assign AddOut_D = (PmSel_S) ? AddTmp_D + AddMux_D : AddTmp_D - $signed(AddMux_D);

  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  // counter
  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

  assign Cnt_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBShift_DI : (~CntZero_S) ? Cnt_DP - 1 : Cnt_DP;

  assign CntZero_S = ~(|Cnt_DP);

  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  // FSM
  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

  always_comb begin : p_fsm
    // default
    State_SN   = State_SP;

    OutVld_SO  = 1'b0;

    LoadEn_S   = 1'b0;

    ARegEn_S   = 1'b0;
    BRegEn_S   = 1'b0;
    ResRegEn_S = 1'b0;

    case (State_SP)
      /////////////////////////////////
      IDLE: begin
        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;

        if (InVld_SI) begin
          OutVld_SO = 1'b0;
          ARegEn_S  = 1'b1;
          BRegEn_S  = 1'b1;
          LoadEn_S  = 1'b1;
          State_SN  = DIVIDE;
        end
      end
      /////////////////////////////////
      DIVIDE: begin

        ARegEn_S   = ABComp_S;
        BRegEn_S   = 1'b1;
        ResRegEn_S = 1'b1;

        // calculation finished
        // one more divide cycle (32nd divide cycle)
        if (CntZero_S) begin
          State_SN = FINISH;
        end
      end
      /////////////////////////////////
      FINISH: begin
        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;

        if (OutRdy_SI) begin
          State_SN = IDLE;
        end
      end
      /////////////////////////////////
      default:  /* default */;
      /////////////////////////////////
    endcase
  end


  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  // regs
  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

  // get flags
  assign RemSel_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpCode_SI[1] : RemSel_SP;
  assign CompInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBSign_SI : CompInv_SP;
  assign ResInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? (~OpBIsZero_SI | OpCode_SI[1]) & OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(
      OpA_DI
  )] ^ OpBSign_SI) : ResInv_SP;

  assign AReg_DN = (ARegEn_S) ? AddOut_D : AReg_DP;
  assign BReg_DN = (BRegEn_S) ? BMux_D : BReg_DP;
  assign ResReg_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? '0 : (ResRegEn_S) ? {
    ABComp_S, ResReg_DP[$high(ResReg_DP):1]
  } : ResReg_DP;

  always_ff @(posedge Clk_CI or negedge Rst_RBI) begin : p_regs
    if (~Rst_RBI) begin
      State_SP   <= IDLE;
      AReg_DP    <= '0;
      BReg_DP    <= '0;
      ResReg_DP  <= '0;
      Cnt_DP     <= '0;
      RemSel_SP  <= 1'b0;
      CompInv_SP <= 1'b0;
      ResInv_SP  <= 1'b0;
    end else begin
      State_SP   <= State_SN;
      AReg_DP    <= AReg_DN;
      BReg_DP    <= BReg_DN;
      ResReg_DP  <= ResReg_DN;
      Cnt_DP     <= Cnt_DN;
      RemSel_SP  <= RemSel_SN;
      CompInv_SP <= CompInv_SN;
      ResInv_SP  <= ResInv_SN;
    end
  end

  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  // assertions
  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

`ifdef CV32E40P_ASSERT_ON
  initial begin : p_assertions
    assert (C_LOG_WIDTH == $clog2(C_WIDTH + 1))
    else $error("C_LOG_WIDTH must be $clog2(C_WIDTH+1)");
  end
`endif

endmodule  // serDiv

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 7/60]
`timescale 1ns/10ps
// Equivalence testbench for cv32e40p_alu, the CV32E40P (RI5CY) execution-stage ALU.
//
// The design under test runs side by side with the upstream ALU, embedded below as cv32e40p_alu__ref
// (the flattened upstream sources of starting_point.sv with every module renamed). Both receive the same
// stimulus, and at every falling clock edge after reset the design's ready_o must equal the reference's:
// an operation is accepted or completed in exactly the cycles the upstream ALU accepts or completes it.
// When the reference signals ready, result_o must equal the reference's, and comparison_result_o as well
// for the 14 comparison opcodes (for every other opcode that output is a don't-care).
//
// Three stimulus phases, all deterministic:
//   1. vectors.dat: 20,000 directed and seeded-random vectors with expected outputs captured from the
//      pristine upstream RTL (fields: op vm bma bmb ive cpx sbr csh a b c cchk exp_result exp_cmp, hex).
//      The reference is also checked against these expected values, which guards the harness itself.
//   2. a divider sweep: every divisor bit length x every dividend bit length x the four div/rem opcodes,
//      twice, with random low bits and random signs for the signed opcodes (8,192 operations).
//   3. N_RANDOM operations from a fixed-seed xorshift generator, within the driven envelope of the task
//      description (opcode set, vector modes per opcode family, modifier flags per opcode).
//
// Protocol (the EX stage's): rst_n low for the first 5 cycles. For each operation all inputs are driven
// at a falling edge with enable_i=1 and ex_ready_i=1 and held; ready_o is polled at the following falling
// edges (cap 64 -> error); the outputs are checked at the first poll where the reference is ready; then
// enable_i is deasserted for one cycle before the next operation. ready_o is compared at every poll and
// in the idle cycle between operations.
module tb;
  logic clk;
  initial clk = 1'b0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  localparam int N_RANDOM = 1_000_000;   // phase 3 length
  localparam int MAX_SHOWN = 10;         // error lines printed; every error is counted

  logic        rst_n, enable_i, ex_ready_i;
  logic [6:0]  op;
  logic [31:0] a, b, c;
  logic [1:0]  vm, ive, csh;
  logic [4:0]  bma, bmb;
  logic        cpx, sbr;
  logic [31:0] result_o, result_ref;
  logic        cmp_o, cmp_ref, ready_o, ready_ref;

  cv32e40p_alu dut (
      .clk(clk), .rst_n(rst_n), .enable_i(enable_i), .operator_i(op),
      .operand_a_i(a), .operand_b_i(b), .operand_c_i(c), .vector_mode_i(vm),
      .bmask_a_i(bma), .bmask_b_i(bmb), .imm_vec_ext_i(ive), .is_clpx_i(cpx), .is_subrot_i(sbr), .clpx_shift_i(csh),
      .result_o(result_o), .comparison_result_o(cmp_o), .ready_o(ready_o), .ex_ready_i(ex_ready_i)
  );
  cv32e40p_alu__ref refm (
      .clk(clk), .rst_n(rst_n), .enable_i(enable_i), .operator_i(op),
      .operand_a_i(a), .operand_b_i(b), .operand_c_i(c), .vector_mode_i(vm),
      .bmask_a_i(bma), .bmask_b_i(bmb), .imm_vec_ext_i(ive), .is_clpx_i(cpx), .is_subrot_i(sbr), .clpx_shift_i(csh),
      .result_o(result_ref), .comparison_result_o(cmp_ref), .ready_o(ready_ref), .ex_ready_i(ex_ready_i)
  );

  // ---- the 57 opcodes (cv32e40p_pkg.sv) and their families ------------------------------------------
  localparam logic [6:0] OPS [0:56] = '{
      7'h18, 7'h19, 7'h1a, 7'h1b, 7'h1c, 7'h1d, 7'h1e, 7'h1f,   // ADD SUB ADDU SUBU ADDR SUBR ADDUR SUBUR
      7'h15, 7'h2e, 7'h2f,                                       // AND OR XOR
      7'h24, 7'h25, 7'h26, 7'h27,                                // SRA SRL ROR SLL
      7'h28, 7'h29, 7'h2a, 7'h2b, 7'h2c, 7'h49,                  // BEXT BEXTU BINS BCLR BSET BREV
      7'h36, 7'h37, 7'h34, 7'h35,                                // FF1 FL1 CNT CLB
      7'h3e, 7'h3f, 7'h2d,                                       // EXTS EXT INS
      7'h00, 7'h01, 7'h04, 7'h05, 7'h08, 7'h09, 7'h0a, 7'h0b, 7'h0c, 7'h0d,   // LTS LTU LES LEU GTS GTU GES GEU EQ NE
      7'h02, 7'h03, 7'h06, 7'h07,                                // SLTS SLTU SLETS SLETU
      7'h10, 7'h11, 7'h12, 7'h13, 7'h14, 7'h16, 7'h17,           // MIN MINU MAX MAXU ABS CLIP CLIPU
      7'h3a, 7'h3b, 7'h38, 7'h39,                                // SHUF SHUF2 PCKLO PCKHI
      7'h30, 7'h31, 7'h32, 7'h33};                               // DIVU DIV REMU REM
  localparam logic [6:0] OP_SUB = 7'h19, OP_ABS = 7'h14, OP_EXTS = 7'h3e, OP_EXT = 7'h3f, OP_INS = 7'h2d;

  function automatic bit is_cmp(input logic [6:0] o);      return o < 7'h0e;                 endfunction
  function automatic bit is_div(input logic [6:0] o);      return o[6:2] == 5'b01100;        endfunction  // DIVU DIV REMU REM
  function automatic bit is_sdiv(input logic [6:0] o);     return o == 7'h31 || o == 7'h33;  endfunction  // DIV REM
  function automatic bit is_addfam(input logic [6:0] o);   return o[6:3] == 4'b0011;         endfunction  // 18..1f
  function automatic bit is_rounding(input logic [6:0] o); return is_addfam(o) && o[2];      endfunction  // ADDR SUBR ADDUR SUBUR
  function automatic bit is_shift(input logic [6:0] o);    return o[6:2] == 5'b01001;        endfunction  // 24..27
  function automatic bit is_bitmanip(input logic [6:0] o); return (o >= 7'h28 && o <= 7'h2c) || o == 7'h49; endfunction
  function automatic bit is_veccmp(input logic [6:0] o);   return is_cmp(o) && !o[1];        endfunction  // not the scalar set-less ops
  function automatic bit is_minmax(input logic [6:0] o);   return o[6:2] == 5'b00100;        endfunction  // 10..13
  function automatic bit is_shuffle(input logic [6:0] o);  return (o >= 7'h38 && o <= 7'h3b) || o == OP_EXTS || o == OP_EXT || o == OP_INS; endfunction
  // vector modes an opcode is driven in: 3 = {32,16,8}, 2 = {16,8}, 1 = {32}
  function automatic int vm_choices(input logic [6:0] o);
    if (is_addfam(o) || is_veccmp(o) || is_minmax(o) || is_shift(o) || o == OP_ABS) return 3;
    if (is_shuffle(o)) return 2;
    return 1;
  endfunction

  // ---- deterministic pseudo-random source (xorshift32, fixed seed) ----------------------------------
  logic [31:0] rs;
  function automatic logic [31:0] xs_next(input logic [31:0] s);
    logic [31:0] x = s;
    x = x ^ (x << 13); x = x ^ (x >> 17); x = x ^ (x << 5);
    return x;
  endfunction
  task automatic draw(output logic [31:0] v);   // advance the generator, return the new state
    rs = xs_next(rs); v = rs;
  endtask
  localparam logic [31:0] CORNERS [0:29] = '{
      32'h00000000, 32'h00000001, 32'h00000002, 32'h00000003, 32'h00000005,
      32'h7FFFFFFF, 32'h80000000, 32'hFFFFFFFF, 32'hFFFFFFFE, 32'h80000001,
      32'h0000FFFF, 32'hFFFF0000, 32'h00010000, 32'h00008000, 32'h00007FFF,
      32'hAAAAAAAA, 32'h55555555, 32'h00FF00FF, 32'hFF00FF00, 32'h01010101,
      32'h7F7F7F7F, 32'h80808080, 32'hFF7F80FE, 32'h0000007F, 32'h00000080,
      32'hDEADBEEF, 32'h12345678, 32'hCAFEF00D, 32'h00000010, 32'hFFFFFF80};
  localparam logic [7:0] LANE_BYTES [0:9] = '{8'h00, 8'h01, 8'h7F, 8'h80, 8'hFF, 8'hFE, 8'h55, 8'hAA, 8'h02, 8'h81};
  // operand distribution of the stimulus generator: raw, corner, byte-lane patterned, small, small negative
  function automatic logic [31:0] rnd32();
    logic [31:0] m, v, k;
    draw(m); m = m % 100;
    draw(v);
    if (m < 35) return v;
    if (m < 55) return CORNERS[v % 30];
    if (m < 75) begin
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin draw(k); v[i*8 +: 8] = LANE_BYTES[k % 10]; end
      return v;
    end
    if (m < 90) return v % 256;
    return -(v % 256);
  endfunction
  function automatic logic [31:0] shift_amt32();
    logic [31:0] m, v;
    draw(m); m = m % 10;
    draw(v);
    case (m)
      0: return 32'd0;  1: return 32'd1;  2: return 32'd7;  3: return 32'd8;  4: return 32'd15;  5: return 32'd16;
      6: return 32'd30; 7: return 32'd31; 8: return v % 32;
      default: return v;                          // amounts beyond 31: only the low bits count per lane
    endcase
  endfunction
  // a value of exactly `bits` significant bits (1..32), random below the leading one
  function automatic logic [31:0] with_bits(input int bits);
    logic [31:0] lead = 32'h1 << (bits - 1);
    logic [31:0] low;
    draw(low);
    return lead | (low & (lead - 1));
  endfunction

  // ---- bookkeeping --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  int total_checks, total_errors, shown, total_cycles, div_cycles;
  bit aborted;

  task automatic note_error(input string kind, input string src, input int idx, input string detail);
    total_errors++;
    if (shown < MAX_SHOWN) begin
      shown++;
      $display("%s %s#%0d op=%02h vm=%0h bma=%02h bmb=%02h ive=%0h cpx=%0h sbr=%0h csh=%0h a=%08h b=%08h c=%08h : %s",
               kind, src, idx, op, vm, bma, bmb, ive, cpx, sbr, csh, a, b, c, detail);
    end
    if (total_errors >= 1000 && !aborted) begin
      aborted = 1'b1;
      $display("TB_ABORT: 1000 errors, the remaining stimulus is skipped");
    end
  endtask

  // apply the operation held in the input signals; compare the handshake every cycle and the outputs when
  // the reference is ready. have_exp: also check the reference against a recorded expectation (phase 1).
  task automatic apply_op(input string src, input int idx, input bit have_exp, input logic [31:0] exp_res, input logic exp_c);
    int cycles; bit lat_bad; string d;
    @(negedge clk);
    if (ready_o !== ready_ref) begin
      d = $sformatf("ready_o=%b in the idle cycle, reference %b", ready_o, ready_ref);
      note_error("TB_EQUIV_ERROR", src, idx, d);
    end
    enable_i = 1'b1;
    cycles = 0; lat_bad = 1'b0;
    do begin
      @(negedge clk);
      cycles++;
      if (!lat_bad && ready_o !== ready_ref) begin
        lat_bad = 1'b1;
        d = $sformatf("ready_o=%b at poll cycle %0d, reference %b (handshake timing differs)", ready_o, cycles, ready_ref);
        note_error("TB_EQUIV_ERROR", src, idx, d);
      end
    end while (ready_ref !== 1'b1 && cycles < 64);
    total_cycles += cycles;
    if (is_div(op)) div_cycles += cycles;
    total_checks++;
    if (ready_ref !== 1'b1) begin
      note_error("TB_HARNESS_ERROR", src, idx, "the reference did not assert ready_o within 64 cycles");
    end else begin
      if (result_o !== result_ref || (is_cmp(op) && cmp_o !== cmp_ref)) begin
        d = $sformatf("expected result=%08h cmp=%s got result=%08h cmp=%b", result_ref,
                      is_cmp(op) ? $sformatf("%b", cmp_ref) : "x", result_o, cmp_o);
        note_error("TB_ERROR", src, idx, d);
      end
      if (have_exp && (result_ref !== exp_res || (is_cmp(op) && cmp_ref !== exp_c))) begin
        d = $sformatf("reference result=%08h cmp=%b differs from the recorded expectation %08h cmp=%b", result_ref, cmp_ref, exp_res, exp_c);
        note_error("TB_HARNESS_ERROR", src, idx, d);
      end
    end
    enable_i = 1'b0;
  endtask

  // one random operation within the driven envelope
  task automatic random_op();
    logic [31:0] r0, r1, m;
    logic [6:0] o;
    int nvm;
    draw(r0); r0 = r0 % 57; o = OPS[r0]; nvm = vm_choices(o);
    op = o;
    draw(r1); r1 = r1 % 3;

... (showing lines 1-200 of 1732; use offset/limit for more)

    draw(r1); r1 = r1 % 3;

      if (o == OP_SUB && vm == 2'b10 && m >= 40 && m < 55) sbr = 1'b1;                       // pv.subrotmj
      else if (!is_rounding(o) && vm != 2'b11 && m >= 55 && m < 70) begin cpx = 1'b1; draw(r0); csh = r0[1:0]; bma = '0; bmb = '0; end
    end

      draw(m); m = m % 10;
      if (o != 7'h2b && o != 7'h2c && o != 7'h49 && m < 7) b = {27'd0, bmb};                 // BEXT/BEXTU/BINS: b = bmb as the decoder does
      if (o == 7'h49) begin draw(r0); r0 = r0 % 32; b = (m < 5) ? r0 : {27'd0, bmb}; end              // BREV: left shift by b[4:0]
    end
    if (is_shift(o) && vm == 2'b00) b = shift_amt32();
    if (o == OP_EXT || o == OP_EXTS || o == OP_INS) begin draw(r0); ive = r0[1:0]; end
    if (o == OP_EXT || o == OP_EXTS) c = '0;
    if (o == OP_ABS) begin b = '0; draw(m); m = m % 5; if (vm == 2'b10 && m == 0) cpx = 1'b1; end   // complex conjugate on 16-bit lanes
    if (o == 7'h16 || o == 7'h17) begin draw(m); draw(r0); r0 = r0 % 32; if (m[0] == 1'b0) b = (32'h1 << r0) - 1; end   // CLIP bounds 2^k-1
    if (is_div(o)) begin
      draw(m); m = m % 10;
      if (m < 4) begin draw(r0); r0 = 1 + r0 % 32; a = with_bits(int'(r0)); end                   // divisor of any bit length
      else if (m < 6) begin draw(a); a = a % 64; end                                              // small divisor, long division
      else if (m < 7) begin draw(r0); draw(r1); b = a * (r0 % 16) + r1 % 8; end                   // near-exact multiple
      if (is_sdiv(o)) begin draw(r0); draw(r1); if (r0[0]) a = -a; if (r1[0]) b = -b; end
    end
  endtask

  // ---- stimulus ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  int fd, n, vidx, k, kb, mb, pass;
  logic [31:0] r0, r1;
  logic [3:0]  cchk, exp_cmp;
  logic [31:0] exp_result;
  string line;

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0; total_errors = 0; shown = 0; total_cycles = 0; div_cycles = 0; aborted = 1'b0; rs = 32'h2545F491;
    rst_n = 1'b0; enable_i = 1'b0; ex_ready_i = 1'b1;
    op = '0; a = '0; b = '0; c = '0; vm = '0; bma = '0; bmb = '0; ive = '0; cpx = 1'b0; sbr = 1'b0; csh = '0;
    repeat (5) @(negedge clk);
    rst_n = 1'b1;
    @(negedge clk);

    // 1. the vector file
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) $fatal(1, "cannot open vectors.dat");
    vidx = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd) && !aborted) begin
      if ($fgets(line, fd) == 0) break;
      if (line.len() == 0 || line[0] == "#" || line[0] == "\n") continue;
      n = $sscanf(line, "%h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h %h",
                  op, vm, bma, bmb, ive, cpx, sbr, csh, a, b, c, cchk, exp_result, exp_cmp);
      if (n != 14) continue;
      apply_op("file", vidx, 1'b1, exp_result, exp_cmp[0]);
      vidx++;
    end
    $fclose(fd);

    // 2. the divider sweep: divisor bits x dividend bits x opcode, two passes
    vidx = 0;
    for (pass = 0; pass < 2 && !aborted; pass++)
      for (k = 53; k < 57 && !aborted; k++)
        for (kb = 1; kb <= 32 && !aborted; kb++)
          for (mb = 1; mb <= 32 && !aborted; mb++) begin
            op = OPS[k]; vm = '0; bma = '0; bmb = '0; ive = '0; cpx = 1'b0; sbr = 1'b0; csh = '0; c = '0;
            a = with_bits(kb);                        // the divisor (operand_a_i)
            b = with_bits(mb);                        // the dividend (operand_b_i)
            if (is_sdiv(op)) begin draw(r0); draw(r1); if (r0[0]) a = -a; if (r1[0]) b = -b; end
            apply_op("sweep", vidx, 1'b0, '0, 1'b0);
            vidx++;
          end

    // 3. random operations within the envelope
    for (vidx = 0; vidx < N_RANDOM && !aborted; vidx++) begin
      random_op();
      apply_op("random", vidx, 1'b0, '0, 1'b0);
    end

    $display("TB_CYCLES total=%0d", total_cycles);
    $display("TB_DIV_CYCLES total=%0d", div_cycles);
    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule
