operand_c_i : {32{extract_sign}};\n\n  assign extract_is_signed = (operator_i == ALU_BEXT);\n  assign extract_sign      = extract_is_signed & shift_result[bmask_a_i];\n\n  assign bextins_result    = (bmask & shift_result) | (bextins_and & bmask_inv);\n\n  assign bclr_result       = operand_a_i & bmask_inv;\n  assign bset_result       = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n  // ---------------- bit reverse ----------------\n  logic [31:0] radix_2_rev;\n  logic [31:0] radix_4_rev;\n  logic [31:0] radix_8_rev;\n  logic [31:0] reverse_result;\n  logic [ 1:0] radix_mux_sel;\n\n  assign radix_mux_sel = bmask_a_i[1:0];\n\n  generate\n    for (j = 0; j < 32; j++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[j] = shift_result[31-j];\n    end\n    for (j = 0; j < 16; j++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*j+1:2*j] = shift_result[31-j*2:31-j*2-1];\n    end\n    for (j = 0; j < 10; j++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*j+2:3*j] = shift_result[31-j*3:31-j*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n\n  always_comb begin\n    reverse_result = '0;\n    unique case (radix_mux_sel)\n      2'b00: reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n      2'b01: reverse_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10: reverse_result = radix_8_rev;\n      default: reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ---------------- divider ----------------\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  logic        div_signed;\n  logic        div_op_a_signed;\n  logic [ 5:0] div_shift_int;\n\n  assign div_signed = operator_i[0];\n\n  assign div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & div_signed;\n\n  assign div_shift_int = ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result;\n  assign div_shift = div_shift_int + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  assign div_valid = enable_i & ((operator_i == ALU_DIV) || (operator_i == ALU_DIVU) ||\n                     (operator_i == ALU_REM) || (operator_i == ALU_REMU));\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (shift_left_result),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI((cnt_result == 0)),\n      .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO(result_div),\n      .InVld_SI (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)\n  );\n\n  // ---------------- result mux ----------------\n  always_comb begin\n    result_o = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_AND: result_o = operand_a_i & operand_b_i;\n      ALU_OR:  result_o = operand_a_i | operand_b_i;\n      ALU_XOR: result_o = operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i;\n\n      ALU_ADD, ALU_ADDR, ALU_ADDU, ALU_ADDUR,\n      ALU_SUB, ALU_SUBR, ALU_SUBU, ALU_SUBUR,\n      ALU_SLL, ALU_SRL, ALU_SRA, ALU_ROR: result_o = shift_result;\n\n      ALU_BINS, ALU_BEXT, ALU_BEXTU: result_o = bextins_result;\n      ALU_BCLR: result_o = bclr_result;\n      ALU_BSET: result_o = bset_result;\n\n      ALU_BREV: result_o = reverse_result;\n\n      ALU_SHUF, ALU_SHUF2, ALU_PCKLO, ALU_PCKHI, ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS, ALU_INS: result_o = pack_result;\n\n      ALU_MIN, ALU_MINU, ALU_MAX, ALU_MAXU: result_o = result_minmax;\n\n      ALU_ABS: result_o = is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]} : result_minmax;\n\n      ALU_CLIP, ALU_CLIPU: result_o = clip_result;\n\n      ALU_EQ, ALU_NE, ALU_GTU, ALU_GEU, ALU_LTU, ALU_LEU, ALU_GTS, ALU_GES, ALU_LTS, ALU_LES: begin\n        result_o[31:24] = {8{cmp_result[3]}};\n        result_o[23:16] = {8{cmp_result[2]}};\n        result_o[15:8]  = {8{cmp_result[1]}};\n        result_o[7:0]   = {8{cmp_result[0]}};\n      end\n\n      ALU_SLTS, ALU_SLTU, ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU: result_o = {31'b0, comparison_result_o};\n\n      ALU_FF1, ALU_FL1, ALU_CLB, ALU_CNT: result_o = {26'h0, bitop_result[5:0]};\n\n      ALU_DIV, ALU_DIVU, ALU_REM, ALU_REMU: result_o = result_div;\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- popcount ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- find first one ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_ff_one #(\n    parameter LEN = 32\n) (\n    input logic [LEN-1:0] in_i,\n    output logic [$clog2(LEN)-1:0] first_one_o,\n    output logic                   no_ones_o\n);\n  localparam NUM_LEVELS = $clog2(LEN);\n\n  logic [          LEN-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_lut;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0]                 sel_nodes;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n  generate\n    genvar j;\n    for (j = 0; j < LEN; j++) begin : gen_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[j] = $unsigned(j);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    genvar k;\n    genvar l;\n    genvar level;\n\n    assign sel_nodes[2**NUM_LEVELS-1] = 1'b0;\n\n    for (level = 0; level < NUM_LEVELS; level++) begin : gen_tree\n      if (level < NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_non_root_level\n        for (l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : gen_node\n          assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+l]   = sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] | sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n          assign index_nodes[2**level-1+l] = (sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] == 1'b1) ?\n                                           index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] : index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n        end\n      end\n      if (level == NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_root_level\n        for (k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : gen_node\n          if (k * 2 < LEN - 1) begin : gen_two\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k] = in_i[k*2] | in_i[k*2+1];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = (in_i[k*2] == 1'b1) ? index_lut[k*2] : index_lut[k*2+1];\n          end\n          if (k * 2 == LEN - 1) begin : gen_one\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = in_i[k*2];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = index_lut[k*2];\n          end\n          if (k * 2 > LEN - 1) begin : gen_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign first_one_o = index_nodes[0];\n  assign no_ones_o   = ~sel_nodes[0];\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- serial divider ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  } State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n\n  assign ABComp_S    = ((AReg_DP == BReg_DP) | ((AReg_DP > BReg_DP) ^ CompInv_SP)) & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n\n  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;\n  assign AddOut_D = (PmSel_S) ? AddTmp_D + AddMux_D : AddTmp_D - $signed(AddMux_D);\n\n  assign Cnt_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBShift_DI : (~CntZero_S) ? OpCode_SI[1] : RemSel_SP;\n  assign CompInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBSign_SI : CompInv_SP;\n  assign ResInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? '0 : (ResRegEn_S) ? {\n    ABComp_S, ResReg_DP[$high(ResReg_DP):1]\n  } : ResReg_DP;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge Clk_CI or negedge Rst_RBI) begin : p_regs\n    if (~Rst_RBI) begin\n      State_SP   <= IDLE;\n      AReg_DP    <= '0;\n      BReg_DP    <= '0;\n      ResReg_DP  <= '0;\n      Cnt_DP     <= '0;\n      RemSel_SP  <= 1'b0;\n      CompInv_SP <= 1'b0;\n      ResInv_SP  <= 1'b0;\n    end else begin\n      State_SP   <= State_SN;\n      AReg_DP    <= AReg_DN;\n      BReg_DP    <= BReg_DN;\n      ResReg_DP  <= ResReg_DN;\n      Cnt_DP     <= Cnt_DN;\n      RemSel_SP  <= RemSel_SN;\n      CompInv_SP <= CompInv_SN;\n      ResInv_SP  <= ResInv_SN;\n    end\n  end\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
The baseline passed with a cost of 645,629 (area 441.5, delay 1462ps). The critical path runs from `operator_i[5]` through deep opcode-decode → shared shifter → result mux chain. My optimization plan:

1. **De-sharing of datapaths**: The upstream uses a single shared shifter (for shifts, adder rounding, bit manipulation, division prep) and one big mux for results — this creates the deep path of decode→mux→shifter→mux. I'll give each opcode family its own direct datapath and use a shallow grouped final mux.
2. **Simplification of shuffle/pack** (upstream computes byte muxes of both registers before selecting).
3. **Cheaper divider zero detection** (a direct `~|a` instead of popcount==0).
4. Keep the divider FSM and all semantics exactly identical.

Writing the restructured design:


  -> create_file(content="// cv32e40p_alu - restructured v2:\n//  - per-family datapaths (no shared shifter muxing), grouped final OR mux\n//  - divider kept cycle-exact (same FSM/datapath as      bmask_a_i,\n    input logic [4:0]         bmask_b_i,\n    input logic [1:0]         imm_vec_ext_i,\n\n    input logic               is_clpx_i,\n    input logic               is_subrot_i,\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SUB   = 7'b0011001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ADDU  = 7'b0011010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SUBU  = 7'b0011011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ADDR  = 7'b0011100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SUBR  = 7'b0011101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ADDUR = 7'b0011110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SUBUR = 7'b0011111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_XOR   = 7'b0101111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_OR    = 7'b0101110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_AND   = 7'b0010101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SRA   = 7'b0100100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SRL   = 7'b0100101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ROR   = 7'b0100110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SLL   = 7'b0100111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BEXT  = 7'b0101000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BEXTU = 7'b0101001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BINS  = 7'b0101010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BCLR  = 7'b0101011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BSET  = 7'b0101100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BREV  = 7'b1001001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_FF1   = 7'b0110110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_FL1   = 7'b0110111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CNT   = 7'b0110100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLB   = 7'b0110101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EXTS  = 7'b0111110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EXT   = 7'b0111111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ABS   = 7'b0010100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLIP  = 7'b0010110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLIPU = 7'b0010111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_INS   = 7'b0101101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MIN   = 7'b0010000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MINU  = 7'b0010001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MAX   = 7'b0010010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MAXU  = 7'b0010011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_DIVU  = 7'b0110000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_DIV   = 7'b0110001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_REMU  = 7'b0110010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_REM   = 7'b0110011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SHUF  = 7'b0111010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SHUF2 = 7'b0111011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_PCKLO = 7'b0111000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_PCKHI = 7'b0111001;\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE16 = 2'b10;\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE8  = 2'b11;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // opcode group decodes\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire mode32 = (vector_mode_i == 2'b00);\n  wire mode16 = (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE16);\n  wire mode8  = (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8);\n\n  wire g_addfam = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0011);          // 0x18..0x1f\n  wire g_shift  = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01001);         // SRA SRL ROR SLL\n  wire g_div    = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01100);         // DIVU DIV REMU REM\n  wire g_bitcnt = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01101);         // CNT CLB FF1 FL1\n  wire g_bmanip5= (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01010) ||       // BEXT..BCLR\n                  (operator_i == ALU_BSET);\n  wire g_brev   = (operator_i == ALU_BREV);\n  wire g_bmanip = g_bmanip5 || g_brev;\n  wire g_cmp    = (operator_i[6:4] == 3'b000);           // 0x00..0x0d\n  wire g_minmax = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00100);         // MIN MINU MAX MAXU\n  wire g_abs    = (operator_i == ALU_ABS);\n  wire g_clip   = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP) || (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU);\n  wire g_shuf   = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01110);         // PCKLO PCKHI SHUF SHUF2\n  wire g_extins = (operator_i == ALU_EXT) || (operator_i == ALU_EXTS) || (operator_i == ALU_INS);\n  wire g_logic  = (operator_i == ALU_AND) || (operator_i == ALU_OR) || (operator_i == ALU_XOR);\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // segmented adder (36-bit trick from upstream, simplified control)\n  // used by: adder family, ABS, CLIP\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire addfam_sub = g_addfam & operator_i[0];            // SUB SUBU SUBR SUBUR\n  wire b_invert   = addfam_sub | is_subrot_i;\n  wire abs_op     = g_abs;\n  wire clip_op    = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP);\n  wire cin0       = b_invert | abs_op | clip_op;\n  // carry blocking: negate-type ops block lane boundaries with cin=1,\n  // plain adds block with cin=0; 32-bit mode always propagates\n  wire block_hi   = (b_invert | abs_op) & (mode16 | mode8); // boundary 16\n  wire block_lo   = (b_invert | abs_op) & mode8;            // boundaries 8,24\n\n  wire [31:0] add_op_a = abs_op ? ~operand_a_i :\n                         is_subrot_i ? {operand_b_i[15:0], operand_a_i[31:16]} : operand_a_i;\n  wire [31:0] add_op_b = b_invert ? (is_subrot_i ? ~{operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[31:16]}\n                                                  : ~operand_b_i)\n                                   : operand_b_i;\n\n  wire [35:0] adder_in_a = {add_op_a[31:24], 1'b1,\n                            add_op_a[23:16], ~block_lo,\n                            add_op_a[15:8],  ~block_hi,\n                            add_op_a[7:0],   1'b1};\n  wire [35:0] adder_in_b = {add_op_b[31:24], 1'b0,\n                            add_op_b[23:16], block_lo,\n                            add_op_b[15:8],  block_hi,\n                            add_op_b[7:0],   cin0};\n\n  wire [36:0] adder_result_expanded = $signed(adder_in_a) + $signed(adder_in_b);\n  wire [31:0] adder_result = {adder_result_expanded[35:28],\n                              adder_result_expanded[26:19],\n                              adder_result_expanded[17:10],\n                              adder_result_expanded[8:1]};\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // bmask (adder rounding + bit manipulation)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] bmask = (~((32'hFFFFFFFE) << bmask_a_i)) << bmask_b_i;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // adder family: rounding + per-lane right shift\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire addfam_round = g_addfam & operator_i[2];          // ADDR SUBR ADDUR SUBUR\n  wire [31:0] round_value = addfam_round ? {1'b0, bmask[31:1]} : 32'h0;\n  wire [31:0] round_sum   = adder_result + round_value;\n\n  wire addfam_arith = ~operator_i[1];                    // ADD SUB ADDR SUBR\n  wire [4:0] fam_sh32 = is_clpx_i ? {3'b000, clpx_shift_i} : bmask_b_i;\n  wire [3:0] fam_sh16 = is_clpx_i ? {2'b00, clpx_shift_i}  : bmask_b_i[3:0];\n  wire [2:0] fam_sh8  = is_clpx_i ? {1'b0, clpx_shift_i}   : bmask_b_i[2:0];\n\n  logic [31:0] addfam_shifted;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (mode16) begin\n      addfam_shifted[31:16] = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[31], round_sum[31:16]}) >>> fam_sh16;\n      addfam_shifted[15:0]  = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[15], round_sum[15:0]})  >>> fam_sh16;\n    end else if (mode8) begin\n      addfam_shifted[31:24] = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[31], round_sum[31:24]}) >>> fam_sh8;\n      addfam_shifted[23:16] = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[23], round_sum[23:16]}) >>> fam_sh8;\n      addfam_shifted[15:8]  = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[15], round_sum[15:8]})  >>> fam_sh8;\n      addfam_shifted[7:0]   = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[7],  round_sum[7:0]})   >>> fam_sh8;\n    end else begin\n      addfam_shifted = $signed({{32{addfam_arith & round_sum[31]}}, round_sum}) >>> fam_sh32;\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // shift ops: SLL SRL SRA ROR (per lane)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] shift_op_result;\n  wire sra_op = (operator_i == ALU_SRA);\n  wire sll_op = (operator_i == ALU_SLL);\n  wire ror_op = (operator_i == ALU_ROR);\n  always_comb begin\n    if (mode16) begin\n      if (sll_op) begin\n        shift_op_result[31:16] = operand_a_i[31:16] << operand_b_i[19:16];\n        shift_op_result[15:0]  = operand_a_i[15:0]  << operand_b_i[3:0];\n      end else begin\n        shift_op_result[31:16] = $signed({sra_op & operand_a_i[31], operand_a_i[31:16]}) >>> operand_b_i[19:16];\n        shift_op_result[15:0]  = $signed({sra_op & operand_a_i[15], operand_a_i[15:0]})  >>> operand_b_i[3:0];\n      end\n    end else if (mode8) begin\n      if (sll_op) begin\n        shift_op_result[31:24] = operand_a_i[31:24] << operand_b_i[26:24];\n        shift_op_result[23:16] = operand_a_i[23:16] << operand_b_i[18:16];\n        shift_op_result[15:8]  = operand_a_i[15:8]  << operand_b_i[10:8];\n        shift_op_result[7:0]   = operand_a_i[7:0]   << operand_b_i[2:0];\n      end else begin\n        shift_op_result[31:24] = $signed({sra_op & operand_a_i[31], operand_a_i[31:24]}) >>> operand_b_i[26:24];\n        shift_op_result[23:16] = $signed({sra_op & operand_a_i[23], operand_a_i[23:16]}) >>> operand_b_i[18:16];\n        shift_op_result[15:8]  = $signed({sra_op & operand_a_i[15], operand_a_i[15:8]})  >>> operand_b_i[10:8];\n        shift_op_result[7:0]   = $signed({sra_op & operand_a_i[7],  operand_a_i[7:0]})   >>> operand_b_i[2:0];\n      end\n    end else begin\n      if (sll_op)       shift_op_result = operand_a_i << operand_b_i[4:0];\n      else if (ror_op)  shift_op_result = (operand_a_i >> operand_b_i[4:0]) |\n                                          (operand_a_i << (6'd32 - {1'b0, operand_b_i[4:0]}));\n      else              shift_op_result = $signed({{32{sra_op & operand_a_i[31]}}, operand_a_i}) >>> operand_b_i[4:0];\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // bit manipulation (32-bit mode)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] bm_shr  = $signed({{(operator_i == ALU_BEXT) & operand_a_i[31] == 1'b1 ?\n                                   32'b0 : 32'b0}}, operand_a_i}) >> 1; // placeholder removed below\n  // right shift (BEXT arithmetic, BEXTU logical) by b[4:0]\n  wire bext_op  = (operator_i == ALU_BEXT);\n  wire [31:0] bm_rshift = bext_op ? ($signed({{32{operand_a_i[31]}}, operand_a_i}) >>> operand_b_i[4:0])\n                                   : (operand_a_i >> operand_b_i[4:0]);\n  wire [31:0] bm_lshift = operand_a_i << operand_b_i[4:0];   // BINS, BREV\n\n  wire        extract_sign = bext_op & bm_rshift[bmask_a_i];\n  wire [31:0] bextins_and  = (operator_i == ALU_BINS) ? operand_c_i : {32{extract_sign}};\n  wire [31:0] bextins_result = (bmask & bm_rshift) | (bextins_and & ~bmask);\n  wire [31:0] bclr_result  = operand_a_i & ~bmask;\n  wire [31:0] bset_result  = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n  // BREV: t = a << b[4:0], reverse in groups by bmask_a_i[1:0]\n  logic [31:0] radix_2_rev, radix_4_rev, radix_8_rev, brev_result;\n  genvar bj;\n  generate\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 32; bj++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[bj] = bm_lshift[31-bj];\n    end\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 16; bj++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*bj+1:2*bj] = bm_lshift[31-bj*2:31-bj*2-1];\n    end\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 10; bj++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*bj+2:3*bj] = bm_lshift[31-bj*3:31-bj*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n  always_comb begin\n    unique case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n      2'b01:   brev_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10:   brev_result = radix_8_rev;\n      default: brev_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  logic [31:0] bmanip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (operator_i == ALU_BCLR)       bmanip_result = bclr_result;\n    else if (operator_i == ALU_BSET)  bmanip_result = bset_result;\n    else if (g_brev)                  bmanip_result = brev_result;\n    else                              bmanip_result = bextins_result;\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // comparisons (vector + scalar set-less) + min/max/abs/clip\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [3:0] cmp_signed;\n  wire [3:0] is_equal_vec;\n  wire [3:0] is_greater_vec;\n\n  // signed-compare ops: LTS LES GTS GES SLTS SLETS (cmp group, op[1:0]==00),\n  // MIN MAX, ABS, CLIP, CLIPU\n  wire cmp_grp_signed = g_cmp & (operator_i[1:0] == 2'b00);\n  wire minmax_signed  = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MAX);\n  wire signed_family  = cmp_grp_signed | minmax_signed | g_abs | g_clip;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    if (signed_family) begin\n      if (mode8)       cmp_signed = 4'b1111;\n      else if (mode16) cmp_signed = 4'b1010;\n      else             cmp_signed = 4'b1000;\n    end else cmp_signed = 4'b0000;\n  end\n\n  genvar ci;\n  generate\n    for (ci = 0; ci < 4; ci++) begin : gen_cmp_vec\n      assign is_equal_vec[ci] = (operand_a_i[8*ci+7:8*ci] == operand_b_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]);\n      assign is_greater_vec[ci] = $signed({operand_a_i[8*ci+7] & cmp_signed[ci], operand_a_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]})\n                                > $signed({operand_b_i[8*ci+7] & cmp_signed[ci], operand_b_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]});\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic [3:0] is_equal, is_greater;\n  always_comb begin\n    // 32-bit defaults\n    is_equal   = {4{is_equal_vec[3] & is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[1] & is_equal_vec[0]}};\n    is_greater = {4{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & (is_greater_vec[2]\n                                       | (is_equal_vec[2] & (is_greater_vec[1]\n                                       | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])))))}};\n    if (mode16) begin\n      is_equal[1:0]   = {2{is_equal_vec[0] & is_equal_vec[1]}};\n      is_equal[3:2]   = {2{is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[3]}};\n      is_greater[1:0] = {2{is_greater_vec[1] | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])}};\n      is_greater[3:2] = {2{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & is_greater_vec[2])}};\n    end else if (mode8) begin\n      is_equal   = is_equal_vec;\n      is_greater = is_greater_vec;\n    end\n  end\n\n  logic [3:0] cmp_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    unique case (operator_i[2:0])\n      3'b000:  cmp_result = ~(is_greater | is_equal);      // LTS SLTS LTU SLTU\n      3'b001:  cmp_result = ~(is_greater | is_equal);      // LTU SLTU\n      3'b100:  cmp_result = ~is_greater;                   // LES SLETS LEU SLETU\n      3'b101:  cmp_result = ~is_greater;                   // LEU SLETU\n      3'b010:  cmp_result = is_greater;                    // GTS SLTS? no -> handled below\n      3'b011:  cmp_result = is_greater;                    // GTU\n      3'b110:  cmp_result = is_greater | is_equal;         // GES SLETS? no -> below\n      3'b111:  cmp_result = is_greater | is_equal;         // GEU\n      default: cmp_result = is_equal;\n    endcase\n    if (operator_i == 7'h02 || operator_i == 7'h03) cmp_result = ~(is_greater | is_equal); // SLTS SLTU\n    if (operator_i == 7'h06 || operator_i == 7'h07) cmp_result = ~is_greater;              // SLETS SLETU\n  end\n\n  assign comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n\n  // min/max/abs\n  wire do_min = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MINU) || g_clip;\n  wire [3:0] sel_minmax = is_greater ^ {4{do_min}};\n  wire [31:0] minmax_b = g_abs ? adder_result : operand_b_i;\n  wire [31:0] result_minmax = {sel_minmax[3] ? operand_a_i[31:24] : minmax_b[31:24],\n                               sel_minmax[2] ? operand_a_i[23:16] : minmax_b[23:16],\n                               sel_minmax[1] ? operand_a_i[15:8]  : minmax_b[15:8],\n                               sel_minmax[0] ? operand_a_i[7:0]   : minmax_b[7:0]};\n\n  // clip\n  wire [31:0] operand_b_neg = ~operand_b_i;\n  wire is_equal_clip = (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) ? (operand_a_i == 32'h0)\n                                                 : (operand_a_i == operand_b_neg);\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    clip_result = result_minmax;\n    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) begin\n      if (operand_a_i[31] || is_equal_clip) clip_result = '0;\n    end else begin\n      if (adder_result_expanded[36] || is_equal_clip) clip_result = operand_b_neg;\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // shuffle / pack / ext / ins (direct implementation)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] shuf_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    shuf_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_PCKLO: shuf_result = mode8 ? {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0]}\n                                      : {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[15:0]};\n      ALU_PCKHI: shuf_result = mode8 ? {operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0], operand_c_i[15:0]}\n                                      : {operand_a_i[31:16], operand_b_i[31:16]};\n      ALU_SHUF: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result = {operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8]   +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0]   +: 8]};\n        end else begin // 16-bit\n          shuf_result = operand_b_i[16] ? {operand_a_i[31:16], operand_a_i[31:16]}\n                                        : {operand_a_i[31:16], operand_a_i[31:16]};\n          shuf_result = {operand_b_i[16] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0],\n                         operand_b_i[0]  ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_SHUF2: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result[31:24] = operand_b_i[26] ? operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[23:16] = operand_b_i[18] ? operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[15:8]  = operand_b_i[10] ? operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[7:0]   = operand_b_i[2]  ? operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0] +: 8];\n        end else begin // 16-bit\n          shuf_result = {operand_b_i[17] ? operand_c_i[31:16] : operand_c_i[15:0],\n                         operand_b_i[17] ? operand_c_i[31:16] : operand_c_i[15:0]};\n          shuf_result = {operand_b_i[17] ? (operand_c_i[31:16]) : (operand_c_i[15:0]),\n                         operand_b_i[1]  ? (operand_c_i[31:16]) : (operand_c_i[15:0])};\n          shuf_result[31:16] = operand_b_i[17] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0];\n          shuf_result[15:0]  = operand_b_i[1]  ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0];\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_EXT: begin\n        if (mode8)      shuf_result = {24'h0, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8]};\n        else            shuf_result = {16'h0, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n      end\n      ALU_EXTS: begin\n        if (mode8)      shuf_result = {{24{operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 1][7]}}, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8]};\n        else            shuf_result = {{16{operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16][15]}}, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n      end\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result = operand_c_i;\n          shuf_result[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8] = operand_a_i[7:0];\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result = imm_vec_ext_i[0] ? {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_c_i[15:0]}\n                                         : {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // bit counting: popcount, ff1/fl1/clb\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [5:0] cnt_result;\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (.in_i(operand_a_i), .result_o(cnt_result));\n\n  // ff tree input: FF1 -> a; FL1/DIVU/REMU -> rev(a); DIV/REM/CLB -> rev(~a) if a[31] else rev(a)\n  wire [31:0] operand_a_rev;\n  genvar ri;\n  generate\n    for (ri = 0; ri < 32; ri++) begin : gen_a_rev\n      assign operand_a_rev[ri] = operand_a_i[31-ri];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire ff1_op = (operator_i == ALU_FF1);\n  wire sign_flip = (g_div & operator_i[0]) | (operator_i == ALU_CLB);  // DIV, REM, CLB\n  wire [31:0] ff_input = ff1_op ? operand_a_i : (operand_a_rev ^ {32{sign_flip & operand_a_i[31]}});\n\n  wire [4:0] ff1_result;\n  wire       ff_no_one;\n  cv32e40p_ff_one ff_one_i (.in_i(ff_input), .first_one_o(ff1_result), .no_ones_o(ff_no_one));\n\n  wire [4:0] fl1_result = 5'd31 - ff1_result;\n  wire [5:0] clb_result = {1'b0, ff1_result} - 6'd1;\n\n  logic [5:0] bitop_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    bitop_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};\n      ALU_FL1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_result};\n      ALU_CNT: bitop_result = cnt_result;\n      ALU_CLB: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? (operand_a_i[31] ? 6'd31 : 6'd0) : clb_result;\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // divider (cycle-exact upstream serial divider)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  wire         div_signed     = operator_i[0];\n  wire         div_op_a_signed= operand_a_i[31] & div_signed;\n  wire [5:0]   div_shift_int  = ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result;\n  wire [5:0]   div_shift      = div_shift_int + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n  wire         div_valid      = enable_i & g_div;\n  wire [31:0]  div_opb        = operand_a_i << div_shift;   // a << div_shift\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (div_opb),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(~|operand_a_i),\n      .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO(result_div),\n      .InVld_SI (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)\n  );\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // result mux (grouped OR)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] logic_result = (operator_i == ALU_AND) ? (is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]}\n                                                  : result_minmax)\n                                    : (g_clip ? clip_result : result_minmax);\n\n  assign result_o = ({32{g_logic}}  & logic_result)\n                  | ({32{g_addfam}} & addfam_shifted)\n                  | ({32{g_shift}}  & shift_op_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bmanip}} & bmanip_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bitcnt}} & {26'h0, bitop_result})\n                  | ({32{g_extins | g_shuf}} & shuf_result)\n                  | ({32{g_cmp & ({32{g_div}} & result_div);\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- popcount ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- find first one (from LSB) ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_ff_one #(\n    parameter LEN = 32\n) (\n    input logic [LEN-1:0] in_i,\n    output logic [$clog2(LEN)-1:0] first_one_o,\n    output logic localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BINS  = 7'b0101010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BCLR  = 7'b0101011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BSET  = 7'b0101100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BREV  = 7'b1001001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_FF1   = 7'b0110110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_FL1   = 7'b0110111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CNT   = 7'b0110100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLB   = 7'b0110101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EXTS  = 7'b0111110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EXT   = 7'b0111111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ABS   = 7'b0010100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLIP  = 7'b0010110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLIPU = 7'b0010111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_INS   = 7'b0101101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MIN   = 7'b0010000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MINU  = 7'b0010001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MAX   = 7'b0010010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MAXU  = 7'b0010011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_DIVU  = 7'b0110000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_DIV   = 7'b0110001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_REMU  = 7'b0110010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_REM   = 7'b0110011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SHUF  = 7'b0111010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SHUF2 = 7'b0111011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_PCKLO = 7'b0111000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_PCKHI = 7'b0111001;\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE16 = 2'b10;\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE8  = 2'b11;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // opcode group decodes\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire mode32 = (vector_mode_i == 2'b00);\n  wire mode16 = (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE16);\n  wire mode8  = (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8);\n\n  wire g_addfam = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0011);          // 0x18..0x1f\n  wire g_shift  = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01001);         // SRA SRL ROR SLL\n  wire g_div    = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01100);         // DIVU DIV REMU REM\n  wire g_bitcnt = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01101);         // CNT CLB FF1 FL1\n  wire g_bmanip5= (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01010) ||       // BEXT BEXTU BINS BCLR\n                  (operator_i == ALU_BSET);\n  wire g_brev   = (operator_i == ALU_BREV);\n  wire g_bmanip = g_bmanip5 || g_brev;\n  wire g_bext   = (operator_i == ALU_BEXT);              // right-shift users\n  wire g_bins   = (operator_i == ALU_BINS);              // left-shift users\n  wire g_cmp    = (operator_i[6:4] == 3'b000);           // 0x00..0x0d\n  wire g_minmax = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00100);         // MIN MINU MAX MAXU\n  wire g_abs    = (operator_i == ALU_ABS);\n  wire g_clip   = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP) || (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU);\n  wire g_shuf   = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01110);         // PCKLO PCKHI SHUF SHUF2\n  wire g_extins = (operator_i == ALU_EXT) || (operator_i == ALU_EXTS) || (operator_i == ALU_INS);\n  wire g_logic  = (operator_i == ALU_AND) || (operator_i == ALU_OR) || (operator_i == ALU_XOR);\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // segmented adder (36-bit trick), used by adder family, ABS, CLIP\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire addfam_sub = g_addfam & operator_i[0];            // SUB SUBU SUBR SUBUR\n  wire b_invert   = addfam_sub | is_subrot_i;\n  wire abs_op     = g_abs;\n  wire clip_op    = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP);\n  wire neg   = b_invert | abs_op | clip_op;\n\n  wire [31:0] add_op_a = abs_op ? ~operand_a_i :\n                         is_subrot_i ? {operand_b_i[15:0], operand_a_i[31:16]} : operand_a_i;\n  wire [31:0] add_op_b = b_invert ? (is_subrot_i ? ~{operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[31:16]}\n                                                  : ~operand_b_i)\n                                   : operand_b_i;\n\n  wire sep0_a  = 1'b1;\n  wire sep9_a  = neg | ~mode8;\n  wire sep18_a = neg | mode32;\n  wire sep27_a = neg | ~mode8;\n  wire sep0_b  = neg;\n  wire sep9_b  = neg & mode8;\n  wire sep18_b = neg & (mode8 | mode16);\n  wire sep27_b = neg & mode8;\n\n  wire [35:0] adder_in_a = {add_op_a[31:24], sep27_a,\n                            add_op_a[23:16], sep18_a,\n                            add_op_a[15:8],  sep9_a,\n                            add_op_a[7:0],   sep0_a};\n  wire [35:0] adder_in_b = {add_op_b[31:24], sep27_b,\n                            add_op_b[23:16], sep18_b,\n                            add_op_b[15:8],  sep9_b,\n                            add_op_b[7:0],   sep0_b};\n\n  wire [36:0] adder_result_expanded = $signed(adder_in_a) + $signed(adder_in_b);\n  wire [31:0] adder_result = {adder_result_expanded[35:28],\n                              adder_result_expanded[26:19],\n                              adder_result_expanded[17:10],\n                              adder_result_expanded[8:1]};\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // bmask + rounding add\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] bmask = (~((32'hFFFFFFFE) << bmask_a_i)) << bmask_b_i;\n  wire addfam_round = g_addfam & operator_i[2];\n  wire [31:0] round_sum = adder_result + (addfam_round ? {1'b0, bmask[31:1]} : 32'h0);\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // shared per-lane right shifter\n  //   adder family: input round_sum, arith=~op[1], amt bmask_b/clpx\n  //   SRA/SRL/ROR : input a,          arith=SRA,    amt b (per lane)\n  //   BEXT/BEXTU  : input a,          arith=BEXT,   amt b[4:0] (32-bit)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire sra_op = (operator_i == ALU_SRA);\n  wire ror32  = (operator_i == ALU_ROR) & mode32;\n  wire addfam_arith = ~operator_i[1];\n  wire rs_arith = g_addfam ? addfam_arith : sra_op | g_bext;\n\n  wire [31:0] rs_in32 = ror32 ? {operand_a_i, operand_a_i} :\n                        (g_addfam ? round_sum : operand_a_i);\n  wire [63:0] rs_in_full = {{32{rs_arith & rs_in32[31]}}, rs_in32};\n\n  wire [4:0] fam_sh32 = is_clpx_i ? {3'b000, clpx_shift_i} : bmask_b_i;\n  wire [3:0] fam_sh16 = is_clpx_i ? {2'b00, clpx_shift_i}  : bmask_b_i[3:0];\n  wire [2:0] fam_sh8  = is_clpx_i ? {1'b0, clpx_shift_i}   : bmask_b_i[2:0];\n\n  logic [31:0] rs_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (mode16) begin\n      rs_result[31:16] = $signed({rs_arith & rs_in32[31], rs_in32[31:16]}) >>>\n                         (g_addfam ? fam_sh16 : operand_b_i[19:16]);\n      rs_result[15:0]  = $signed({rs_arith & rs_in32[15], rs_in32[15:0]}) >>>\n                         (g_addfam ? fam_sh16 : operand_b_i[3:0]);\n    end else if (mode8) begin\n      rs_result[31:24] = $signed({rs_arith & rs_in32[31], rs_in32[31:24]}) >>>\n                         (g_addfam ? fam_sh8 : operand_b_i[26:24]);\n      rs_result[23:16] = $signed({rs_arith & rs_in32[23], rs_in32[23:16]}) >>>\n                         (g_addfam ? fam_sh8 : operand_b_i[18:16]);\n      rs_result[15:8]  = $signed({rs_arith & rs_in32[15], rs_in32[15:8]}) >>>\n                         (g_addfam ? fam_sh8 : operand_b_i[10:8]);\n      rs_result[7:0]   = $signed({rs_arith & rs_in32[7],  rs_in32[7:0]}) >>>\n                         (g_addfam ? fam_sh8 : operand_b_i[2:0]);\n    end else begin\n      rs_result = rs_in_full >> (g_addfam ? fam_sh32 : operand_b_i[4:0]);\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // shared per-lane left shifter (SLL) / 32-bit left shift (BINS/BREV/div)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] ls_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (mode16 & ~g_div & ~g_bins & ~g_brev) begin\n      ls_result[31:16] = operand_a_i[31:16] << operand_b_i[19:16];\n      ls_result[15:0]  = operand_a_i[15:0]  << operand_b_i[3:0];\n    end else if (mode8 & ~g_div & ~g_bins & ~g_brev) begin\n      ls_result[31:24] = operand_a_i[31:24] << operand_b_i[26:24];\n      ls_result[23:16] = operand_a_i[23:16] << operand_b_i[18:16];\n      ls_result[15:8]  = operand_a_i[15:8]  << operand_b_i[10:8];\n      ls_result[7:0]   = operand_a_i[7:0]   << operand_b_i[2:0];\n    end else begin\n      ls_result = operand_a_i << operand_b_i[4:0];\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // bit manipulation (32-bit mode)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire        extract_sign   = g_bext & rs_result[bmask_a_i];\n  wire [31:0] bextins_and    = g_bins ? operand_c_i : {32{extract_sign}};\n  wire [31:0] bextins_result = (bmask & (g_bins ? ls_result : rs_result))\n                             | (bextins_and & ~bmask);\n  wire [31:0] bclr_result    = operand_a_i & ~bmask;\n  wire [31:0] bset_result    = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n  // BREV: t = a << b[4:0] (ls_result in 32-bit mode), reverse in groups\n  logic [31:0] radix_2_rev, radix_4_rev, radix_8_rev, brev_result;\n  genvar bj;\n  generate\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 32; bj++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[bj] = ls_result[31-bj];\n    end\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 16; bj++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*bj+1:2*bj] = ls_result[31-bj*2:31-bj*2-1];\n    end\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 10; bj++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*bj+2:3*bj] = ls_result[31-bj*3:31-bj*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n  always_comb begin\n    unique case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n      2'b01:   brev_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10:   brev_result = radix_8_rev;\n      default: brev_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  logic [31:0] bmanip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (operator_i == ALU_BCLR)       bmanip_result = bclr_result;\n    else if (operator_i == ALU_BSET)  bmanip_result = bset_result;\n    else if (g_brev)                  bmanip_result = brev_result;\n    else                              bmanip_result = bextins_result;\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // comparisons + min/max/abs/clip\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [3:0] cmp_signed;\n  wire [3:0] is_equal_vec;\n  wire [3:0] is_greater_vec;\n\n  wire cmp_grp_signed = g_cmp & ~operator_i[0];\n  wire minmax_signed  = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MAX);\n  wire signed_family  = cmp_grp_signed | minmax_signed | g_abs | g_clip;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    if (signed_family) begin\n      if (mode8)       cmp_signed = 4'b1111;\n      else if (mode16) cmp_signed = 4'b1010;\n      else             cmp_signed = 4'b1000;\n    end else cmp_signed = 4'b0000;\n  end\n\n  genvar ci;\n  generate\n    for (ci = 0; ci < 4; ci++) begin : gen_cmp_vec\n      assign is_equal_vec[ci] = (operand_a_i[8*ci+7:8*ci] == operand_b_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]);\n      assign is_greater_vec[ci] = $signed({operand_a_i[8*ci+7] & cmp_signed[ci], operand_a_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]})\n                                > $signed({operand_b_i[8*ci+7] & cmp_signed[ci], operand_b_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]});\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic [3:0] is_equal, is_greater;\n  always_comb begin\n    is_equal   = {4{is_equal_vec[3] & is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[1] & is_equal_vec[0]}};\n    is_greater = {4{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & (is_greater_vec[2]\n                                       | (is_equal_vec[2] & (is_greater_vec[1]\n                                       | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])))))}};\n    if (mode16) begin\n      is_equal[1:0]   = {2{is_equal_vec[0] & is_equal_vec[1]}};\n      is_equal[3:2]   = {2{is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[3]}};\n      is_greater[1:0] = {2{is_greater_vec[1] | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])}};\n      is_greater[3:2] = {2{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & is_greater_vec[2])}};\n    end else if (mode8) begin\n      is_equal   = is_equal_vec;\n      is_greater = is_greater_vec;\n    end\n  end\n\n  logic [3:0] cmp_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    unique case (operator_i[6:2])\n      5'b00000: cmp_result = ~(is_greater | is_equal);     // LTS LTU SLTS SLTU\n      5'b00001: cmp_result = ~is_greater;                  // LES LEU SLETS SLETU\n      5'b00010: cmp_result = operator_i[1] ? (is_greater | is_equal) : is_greater; // GES GEU / GTS GTU\n      5'b00011: cmp_result = operator_i[0] ? ~is_equal : is_equal;                 // NE / EQ\n      default:  cmp_result = is_equal;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n\n  wire do_min = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MINU) || g_clip;\n  wire [3:0] sel_minmax = is_greater ^ {4{do_min}};\n  wire [31:0] minmax_b = g_abs ? adder_result : operand_b_i;\n  wire [31:0] result_minmax = {sel_minmax[3] ? operand_a_i[31:24] : minmax_b[31:24],\n                               sel_minmax[2] ? operand_a_i[23:16] : minmax_b[23:16],\n                               sel_minmax[1] ? operand_a_i[15:8]  : minmax_b[15:8],\n                               sel_minmax[0] ? operand_a_i[7:0]   : minmax_b[7:0]};\n\n  wire [31:0] operand_b_neg = ~operand_b_i;\n  wire is_equal_clip = (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) ? (operand_a_i == 32'h0)\n                                                 : (operand_a_i == operand_b_neg);\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    clip_result = result_minmax;\n    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) begin\n      if (operand_a_i[31] || is_equal_clip) clip_result = '0;\n    end else begin\n      if (adder_result_expanded[36] || is_equal_clip) clip_result = operand_b_neg;\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // shuffle / pack / ext / ins\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] shuf_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    shuf_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_PCKLO: shuf_result = mode8 ? {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0]}\n                                      : {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[15:0]};\n      ALU_PCKHI: shuf_result = mode8 ? {operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0], operand_c_i[15:0]}\n                                      : {operand_a_i[31:16], operand_b_i[31:16]};\n      ALU_SHUF: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result = {operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8]   +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0]   +: 8]};\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result = {operand_b_i[16] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0],\n                         operand_b_i[0]  ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_SHUF2: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result[31:24] = operand_b_i[26] ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[23:16] = operand_b_i[18] ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[15:8]  = operand_b_i[10] ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[7:0]   = operand_b_i[2]  ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0] +: 8];\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result[31:16] = operand_b_i[17] ? (operand_b_i[16] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0])\n                                               : (operand_b_i[16] ? operand_c_i[31:16] : operand_c_i[15:0]);\n          shuf_result[15:0]  = operand_b_i[1]  ? (operand_b_i[0] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0])\n                                               : (operand_b_i[0] ? operand_c_i[31:16] : operand_c_i[15:0]);\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_EXT: begin\n        if (mode8)      shuf_result = {24'h0, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8]};\n        else            shuf_result = {16'h0, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n      end\n      ALU_EXTS: begin\n        if (mode8)      shuf_result = {{24{operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i + 7]}}, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8]};\n        else            shuf_result = {{16{operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] + 15]}}, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n      end\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result = operand_c_i;\n          shuf_result[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8] = operand_a_i[7:0];\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result = imm_vec_ext_i[0] ? {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_c_i[15:0]}\n                                         : {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // bit counting\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [5:0] cnt_result;\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (.in_i(operand_a_i), .result_o(cnt_result));\n\n  wire [31:0] operand_a_rev;\n  genvar ri;\n  generate\n    for (ri = 0; ri < 32; ri++) begin : gen_a_rev\n      assign operand_a_rev[ri] = operand_a_i[31-ri];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire ff1_op = (operator_i == ALU_FF1);\n  wire sign_flip = (g_div & operator_i[0]) | (operator_i == ALU_CLB);\n  wire [31:0] ff_input = ff1_op ? operand_a_i : (operand_a_rev ^ {32{sign_flip & operand_a_i[31]}});\n\n  // one-hot ff1: isolate lowest set bit, then encode\n  wire [31:0] ff_onehot = ff_input & (~ff_input + 32'd1);\n  wire        ff_no_one = ~|ff_input;\n  wire [4:0]  ff1_result;\n  genvar ei, ek;\n  generate\n    for (ek = 0; ek < 5; ek++) begin : gen_enc\n      logic [15:0] enc_sel;\n      always_comb begin\n        for (ei = 0; ei < 16; ei++) enc_sel[ei] = ff_onehot[(ei << 1) | {4'h0, 1'b0} | ek];\n      end\n      assign ff1_result[ek] = |enc_sel;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [4:0] fl1_result = 5'd31 - ff1_result;\n  wire [5:0] clb_result = ff1_result - 5'd1;\n\n  logic [5:0] bitop_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    bitop_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};\n      ALU_FL1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_result};\n      ALU_CNT: bitop_result = cnt_result;\n      ALU_CLB: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? (operand_a_i[31] ? 6'd31 : 6'd0) : clb_result;\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // divider (cycle-exact upstream serial divider)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  wire         div_signed      = operator_i[0];\n  wire         div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & div_signed;\n  wire [5:0]   div_shift_int   = ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result;\n  wire [5:0]   div_shift       = div_shift_int + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n  wire         div_valid       = enable_i & g_div;\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (ls_result),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(ff_no_one),\n      .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO(result_div),\n      .InVld_SI (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)\n  );\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // result mux (grouped OR)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] logic_result = (operator_i == ALU_AND) ? (operand_a_i & operand_b_i) :\n                             (operator_i == ALU_OR)  ? (operand_a_i | operand_b_i) :\n                                                       (operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i);\n\n  wire [31:0] cmp_fill = {{8{cmp_result[3]}}, {8{cmp_result[2]}}, {8{cmp_result[1]}}, {8{cmp_result[0]}}};\n  wire scalar_setless = g_cmp & operator_i[1] & ~operator_i[3];\n\n  wire [31:0] minmax_result = g_abs ? (is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]}\n                                                  : result_minmax)\n                                    : (g_clip ? clip_result : result_minmax);\n\n  wire g_right_shift = g_addfam | g_shift | g_bext | (operator_i == ALU_BEXTU);\n\n  assign result_o = ({32{g_logic}}  & logic_result)\n                  | ({32{g_right_shift}} & rs_result)\n                  | ({32{(operator_i == ALU_SLL)}} & ls_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bmanip}} & bmanip_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bitcnt}} & {26'h0, bitop_result})\n                  | ({32{g_extins | g_shuf}} & shuf_result)\n                  | ({32{g_cmp & ~scalar_setless}} & cmp_fill)\n                  | ({32{scalar_setless}} & {31'b0, cmp_result[3]})\n                  | ({32{g_minmax | g_abs | g_clip}} & minmax_result)\n                  | ({32{g_div}} & result_div);\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- popcount ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- serial divider (upstream, unchanged) ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  } State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ?                        > $signed({operand_b_i[8*ci+7] & cmp_signed[ci], operand_b_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]});\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic [3:0] is_equal, is_greater;\n  always_comb begin\n    is_equal   = {4{is_equal_vec[3] & is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[1] & is_equal_vec[0]}};\n    is_greater = {4{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & (is_greater_vec[2]\n                                       | {2{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & is_greater_vec[2])}};\n    end else if (mode8) begin\n      is_equal   = is_equal_vec;\n      is_greater = is_greater_vec;\n    end\n  end\n\n  logic [3:0] cmp_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    unique case (operator_i[6:2])\n      5'b00000: cmp_result = ~(is_greater | is_equal);\n      5'b00001: cmp_result = ~is_greater;\n      5'b00010: cmp_result = operator_i[1] ? v4 passes at 673,282 — closer but still above baseline (645,629). Key observations:
1. My shifters use explicit 64-bit sign-extended barrels (`{{32{...}}, x} >>> amt`) where 32-bit arithmetic shifts would suffice — likely a large area waste.
2. The divider computes a separate 32-bit comparator AND subtractor; since the divider always subtracts during DIVIDE cycles (PmSel only active during load), the comparator can be derived from the subtractor's carry-out.

Let me build design5 with these fixes:

[ASSISTANT]
Classic SystemVerilog context bug: `cond ? ($signed(x) >>> n) : (x >> n)` evaluates `>>>` in an unsigned context, making it a logical shift. Fix: compute arith/logical results as separate continuous assigns, then mux.
