[ASSISTANT]
Key insights gathered:
- Baseline: 645k (441 area, 1462ps). Increasing target_delay doesn't improve ADP; lowering it doesn't change anything either.
- v4 (decoupled shifter): delay 1226 but area 549 → 673k. Need to reduce area while keeping delay around 1200.
- The divider comparator trick in design5 worked functionally and eliminates the wide compare tree.

Next, I'll build v6 by merging v4's two right-shift barrels into one (with a fast `g_addfam` input mux), keeping the left-shift barrel and the cheaper divider compare:


  -> create_file(content="// cv32e40p_alu - v6: two barrels only:\n//  Barrel R (right): adder-family + shift ops + BEXT/BEXTU; input mux only on g_addfam\n//  Barrel L (left via bit-reversal): SLL, BINS, BREV, div prep\n//  divider comparator derived from its subtractor carry-out\n//  one-hot ff1, grouped OR result mux\n\nmodule cv32e40p_alu\n(\n    input logic               clk,\n    input logic               rst_n,\n    input logic               enable_i,\n    input logic [ 6:0]        operator_i,\n    input logic [31:0]        operand_a_i,\n    input logic [31:0]        operand_b_i,\n    input logic [31:0]        operand_c_i,\n\n    input logic [1:0]         vector_mode_i,\n    input logic [4:0]         bmask_a_i,\n    input logic [4:0]         bmask_b_i,\n    input logic [1:0]         imm_vec_ext_i,\n\n    input logic               is_clpx_i,\n    input logic               is_subrot_i,\n    input logic [1:0]         clpx_shift_i,\n\n    output logic [31:0]       result_o,\n    output logic              comparison_result_o,\n\n    output logic              ready_o,\n    input  logic              ex_ready_i\n);\n\n  /* verilator lint_off WIDTHTRUNC */\n  /* verilator lint_off CASEINCOMPLETE */\n  /* verilator lint_off UNUSEDSIGNAL */\n\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ADD   = 7'b0011000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_XOR   = 7'b0101111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_OR    = 7'b0101110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_AND   = 7'b0010101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SRA   = 7'b0100100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SRL   = 7'b0100101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ROR   = 7'b0100110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SLL   = 7'b0100111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BEXT  = 7'b0101000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BEXTU = 7'b0101001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BINS  = 7'b0101010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BCLR  = 7'b0101011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BSET  = 7'b0101100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_BREV  = 7'b1001001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_FF1   = 7'b0110110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_FL1   = 7'b0110111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CNT   = 7'b0110100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLB   = 7'b0110101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EXTS  = 7'b0111110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EXT   = 7'b0111111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ABS   = 7'b0010100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLIP  = 7'b0010110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLIPU = 7'b0010111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_INS   = 7'b0101101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MIN   = 7'b0010000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MINU  = 7'b0010001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MAX   = 7'b0010010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MAXU  = 7'b0010011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SHUF  = 7'b0111010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SHUF2 = 7'b0111011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_PCKLO = 7'b0111000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_PCKHI = 7'b0111001;\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE16 = 2'b10;\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE8  = 2'b11;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // opcode group decodes\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire mode32 = (vector_mode_i == 2'b00);\n  wire mode16 = (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE16);\n  wire mode8  = (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8);\n\n  wire g_addfam = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0011);\n  wire g_shift  = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01001);         // SRA SRL ROR SLL\n  wire g_div    = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01100);         // DIVU DIV REMU REM\n  wire g_bitcnt = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01101);         // CNT CLB FF1 FL1\n  wire g_brev   = (operator_i == ALU_BREV);\n  wire g_bmanip = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01010) ||       // BEXT BEXTU BINS BCLR\n                  (operator_i == ALU_BSET) || g_brev;\n  wire g_bext   = (operator_i == ALU_BEXT);\n  wire g_bins   = (operator_i == ALU_BINS);\n  wire g_cmp    = (operator_i[6:4] == 3'b000);\n  wire g_minmax = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00100);\n  wire g_abs    = (operator_i == ALU_ABS);\n  wire g_clip   = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP) || (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU);\n  wire g_shuf   = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01110);\n  wire g_extins = (operator_i == ALU_EXT) || (operator_i == ALU_EXTS) || (operator_i == ALU_INS);\n  wire g_logic  = (operator_i == ALU_AND) || (operator_i == ALU_OR) || (operator_i == ALU_XOR);\n\n  wire sll_op = (operator_i == ALU_SLL);\n  wire sra_op = (operator_i == ALU_SRA);\n  wire ror_op = (operator_i == ALU_ROR);\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // segmented adder (adder family, ABS, CLIP)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire addfam_sub = g_addfam & operator_i[0];\n  wire b_invert   = addfam_sub | is_subrot_i;\n  wire abs_op     = g_abs;\n  wire clip_op    = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP);\n  wire neg        = b_invert | abs_op | clip_op;\n\n  wire [31:0] add_op_a = abs_op ? ~operand_a_i :\n                         is_subrot_i ? {operand_b_i[15:0], operand_a_i[31:16]} : operand_a_i;\n  wire [31:0] add_op_b = b_invert ? (is_subrot_i ? ~{operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[31:16]}\n                                                  : ~operand_b_i)\n                                   : operand_b_i;\n\n  wire sep9_a  = neg | ~mode8;\n  wire sep18_a = neg | mode32;\n  wire sep27_a = neg | ~mode8;\n  wire sep0_b  = neg;\n  wire sep9_b  = neg & mode8;\n  wire sep18_b = neg & (mode8 | mode16);\n  wire sep27_b = neg & mode8;\n\n  wire [35:0] adder_in_a = {add_op_a[31:24], sep27_a,\n                            add_op_a[23:16], sep18_a,\n                            add_op_a[15:8],  sep9_a,\n                            add_op_a[7:0],   1'b1};\n  wire [35:0] adder_in_b = {add_op_b[31:24], sep27_b,\n                            add_op_b[23:16], sep18_b,\n                            add_op_b[15:8],  sep9_b,\n                            add_op_b[7:0],   sep0_b};\n\n  wire [36:0] adder_result_expanded = $signed(adder_in_a) + $signed(adder_in_b);\n  wire [31:0] adder_result = {adder_result_expanded[35:28],\n                              adder_result_expanded[26:19],\n                              adder_result_expanded[17:10],\n                              adder_result_expanded[8:1]};\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // rounding add (adder family)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] bmask = (~((32'hFFFFFFFE) << bmask_a_i)) << bmask_b_i;\n  wire addfam_round = operator_i[2];   // meaningful within g_addfam\n  wire [31:0] round_sum = adder_result + (addfam_round ? {1'b0, bmask[31:1]} : 32'h0);\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Barrel R: per-lane right shifter\n  //   data  = round_sum (adder family) or operand_a\n  //   arith = ~op[1] (adder family) or SRA/BEXT\n  //   amt   = bmask/clpx (adder family) or operand_b lanes\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire addfam_arith = ~operator_i[1];\n  wire rs_arith = g_addfam ? addfam_arith : (sra_op | g_bext);\n  wire ror32    = ror_op & mode32;\n\n  wire [4:0] fam_sh32 = is_clpx_i ? {3'b000, clpx_shift_i} : bmask_b_i;\n  wire [3:0] fam_sh16 = is_clpx_i ? {2'b00, clpx_shift_i}  : bmask_b_i[3:0];\n  wire [2:0] fam_sh8  = is_clpx_i ? {1'b0, clpx_shift_i}   : bmask_b_i[2:0];\n\n  wire [31:0] rs_in32 = g_addfam ? round_sum : operand_a_i;\n  wire [63:0] rs_in_full = ror32 ? {operand_a_i, operand_a_i}\n                                 : {{32{rs_arith & rs_in32[31]}}, rs_in32};\n\n  logic [31:0] rs_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (mode16) begin\n      rs_result[31:16] = $signed({rs_arith & rs_in32[31], rs_in32[31:16]}) >>>\n                         (g_addfam ? fam_sh16 : operand_b_i[19:16]);\n      rs_result[15:0]  = $signed({rs_arith & rs_in32[15], rs_in32[15:0]}) >>>\n                         (g_addfam ? fam_sh16 : operand_b_i[3:0]);\n    end else if (mode8) begin\n      rs_result[31:24] = $signed({rs_arith & rs_in32[31], rs_in32[31:24]}) >>>\n                         (g_addfam ? fam_sh8 : operand_b_i[26:24]);\n      rs_result[23:16] = $signed({rs_arith & rs_in32[23], rs_in32[23:16]}) >>>\n                         (g_addfam ? fam_sh8 : operand_b_i[18:16]);\n      rs_result[15:8]  = $signed({rs_arith & rs_in32[15], rs_in32[15:8]}) >>>\n                         (g_addfam ? fam_sh8 : operand_b_i[10:8]);\n      rs_result[7:0]   = $signed({rs_arith & rs_in32[7],  rs_in32[7:0]}) >>>\n                         (g_addfam ? fam_sh8 : operand_b_i[2:0]);\n    end else begin\n      rs_result = rs_in_full >> (g_addfam ? fam_sh32 : operand_b_i[4:0]);\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Barrel L: right shift of bit-reversed data => left shift\n  //   users: SLL (per lane), BINS/BREV (32-bit), div divisor prep\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] operand_a_rev;\n  genvar ri;\n  generate\n    for (ri = 0; ri < 32; ri++) begin : gen_a_rev\n      assign operand_a_rev[ri] = operand_a_i[31-ri];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ff1 machinery (bit-count ops + div_shift)\n  wire ff1_op = (operator_i == ALU_FF1);\n  wire sign_flip = (g_div & operator_i[0]) | (operator_i == ALU_CLB);\n  wire [31:0] ff_input = ff1_op ? operand_a_i : (operand_a_rev ^ {32{sign_flip & operand_a_i[31]}});\n  wire [31:0] ff_onehot = ff_input & (~ff_input + 32'd1);\n  wire        ff_no_one = ~|ff_input;\n  logic [4:0] ff1_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    ff1_result = 5'd0;\n    for (int i = 1; i < 32; i++)\n      if (ff_onehot[i]) ff1_result = i[4:0];\n  end\n  wire [5:0] clb_result = ff1_result - 5'd1;\n\n  wire         div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & operator_i[0];\n  wire [5:0]   div_shift_int   = ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result;\n  wire [5:0]   div_shift       = div_shift_int + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  // reversed shift amount for per-lane left shifts\n  logic [31:0] ls_amt_rev;\n  wire [31:0] ls_amt_src = g_div ? {26'b0, div_shift[4:0]} : operand_b_i;\n  always_comb begin\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: ls_amt_rev = {ls_amt_src[15:0], ls_amt_src[31:16]};\n      VEC_MODE8:  ls_amt_rev = {ls_amt_src[7:0], ls_amt_src[15:8], ls_amt_src[23:16], ls_amt_src[31:24]};\n      default:    ls_amt_rev = ls_amt_src;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  logic [31:0] rs_l_out;   // right-shift of reversed data\n  always_comb begin\n    if (mode16 & sll_op) begin\n      rs_l_out[31:16] = operand_a_rev[31:16] >> ls_amt_rev[19:16];\n      rs_l_out[15:0]  = operand_a_rev[15:0]  >> ls_amt_rev[3:0];\n    end else if (mode8 & sll_op) begin\n      rs_l_out[31:24] = operand_a_rev[31:24] >> ls_amt_rev[26:24];\n      rs_l_out[23:16] = operand_a_rev[23:16] >> ls_amt_rev[18:16];\n      rs_l_out[15:8]  = operand_a_rev[15:8]  >> ls_amt_rev[10:8];\n      rs_l_out[7:0]   = operand_a_rev[7:0]   >> ls_amt_rev[2:0];\n    end else begin\n      rs_l_out = operand_a_rev >> ls_amt_rev[4:0];\n    end\n  end\n\n  wire [31:0] shift_left_result;\n  generate\n    for (ri = 0; ri < 32; ri++) begin : gen_left_result\n      assign shift_left_result[ri] = rs_l_out[31-ri];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // bit manipulation (32-bit mode)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire        extract_sign   = g_bext & rs_result[bmask_a_i];\n  wire [31:0] bextins_and    = g_bins ? operand_c_i : {32{extract_sign}};\n  wire [31:0] bextins_result = (bmask & (g_bins ? shift_left_result : rs_result))\n                             | (bextins_and & ~bmask);\n  wire [31:0] bclr_result    = operand_a_i & ~bmask;\n  wire [31:0] bset_result    = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n  logic [31:0] radix_2_rev, radix_4_rev, radix_8_rev, brev_result;\n  genvar bj;\n  generate\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 32; bj++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[bj] = shift_left_result[31-bj];\n    end\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 16; bj++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*bj+1:2*bj] = shift_left_result[31-bj*2:31-bj*2-1];\n    end\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 10; bj++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*bj+2:3*bj] = shift_left_result[31-bj*3:31-bj*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n  always_comb begin\n    unique case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n      2'b01:   brev_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10:   brev_result = radix_8_rev;\n      default: brev_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  logic [31:0] bmanip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (operator_i == ALU_BCLR)       bmanip_result = bclr_result;\n    else if (operator_i == ALU_BSET)  bmanip_result = bset_result;\n    else if (g_brev)                  bmanip_result = brev_result;\n    else                              bmanip_result = bextins_result;\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // comparisons + min/max/abs/clip\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [3:0] cmp_signed;\n  wire [3:0] is_equal_vec;\n  wire [3:0] is_greater_vec;\n\n  wire cmp_grp_signed = g_cmp & ~operator_i[0];\n  wire minmax_signed  = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MAX);\n  wire signed_family  = cmp_grp_signed | minmax_signed | g_abs | g_clip;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    if (signed_family) begin\n      if (mode8)       cmp_signed = 4'b1111;\n      else if (mode16) cmp_signed = 4'b1010;\n      else             cmp_signed = 4'b1000;\n    end else cmp_signed = 4'b0000;\n  end\n\n  genvar ci;\n  generate\n    for (ci = 0; ci < 4; ci++) begin : gen_cmp_vec\n      assign is_equal_vec[ci] = (operand_a_i[8*ci+7:8*ci] == operand_b_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]);\n      assign is_greater_vec[ci] = $signed({operand_a_i[8*ci+7] & cmp_signed[ci], operand_a_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]})\n                                > $signed({operand_b_i[8*ci+7] & cmp_signed[ci], operand_b_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]});\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic [3:0] is_equal, is_greater;\n  always_comb begin\n    is_equal   = {4{is_equal_vec[3] & is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[1] & is_equal_vec[0]}};\n    is_greater = {4{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & (is_greater_vec[2]\n                                       | (is_equal_vec[2] & (is_greater_vec[1]\n                                       | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])))))}};\n    if (mode16) begin\n      is_equal[1:0]   = {2{is_equal_vec[0] & is_equal_vec[1]}};\n      is_equal[3:2]   = {2{is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[3]}};\n      is_greater[1:0] = {2{is_greater_vec[1] | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])}};\n      is_greater[3:2] = {2{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & is_greater_vec[2])}};\n    end else if (mode8) begin\n      is_equal   = is_equal_vec;\n      is_greater = is_greater_vec;\n    end\n  end\n\n  logic [3:0] cmp_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    unique case (operator_i[6:2])\n      5'b00000: cmp_result = ~(is_greater | is_equal);\n      5'b00001: cmp_result = ~is_greater;\n      5'b00010: cmp_result = operator_i[1] ? (is_greater | is_equal) : is_greater;\n      5'b00011: cmp_result = operator_i[0] ? ~is_equal : is_equal;\n      default:  cmp_result = is_equal;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n\n  wire do_min = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MINU) || g_clip;\n  wire [3:0] sel_minmax = is_greater ^ {4{do_min}};\n  wire [31:0] minmax_b = g_abs ? adder_result : operand_b_i;\n  wire [31:0] result_minmax = {sel_minmax[3] ? operand_a_i[31:24] : minmax_b[31:24],\n                               sel_minmax[2] ? operand_a_i[23:16] : minmax_b[23:16],\n                               sel_minmax[1] ? operand_a_i[15:8]  : minmax_b[15:8],\n                               sel_minmax[0] ? operand_a_i[7:0]   : minmax_b[7:0]};\n\n  wire [31:0] operand_b_neg = ~operand_b_i;\n  wire is_equal_clip = (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) ? (operand_a_i == 32'h0)\n                                                 : (operand_a_i == operand_b_neg);\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    clip_result = result_minmax;\n    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) begin\n      if (operand_a_i[31] || is_equal_clip) clip_result = '0;\n    end else begin\n      if (adder_result_expanded[36] || is_equal_clip) clip_result = operand_b_neg;\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // shuffle / pack / ext / ins\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] shuf_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    shuf_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_PCKLO: shuf_result = mode8 ? {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0]}\n                                      : {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[15:0]};\n      ALU_PCKHI: shuf_result = mode8 ? {operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0], operand_c_i[15:0]}\n                                      : {operand_a_i[31:16], operand_b_i[31:16]};\n      ALU_SHUF: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result = {operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8]   +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0]   +: 8]};\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result = {operand_b_i[16] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0],\n                         operand_b_i[0]  ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_SHUF2: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result[31:24] = operand_b_i[26] ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[23:16] = operand_b_i[18] ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[15:8]  = operand_b_i[10] ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[7:0]   = operand_b_i[2]  ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0] +: 8];\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result[31:16] = operand_b_i[17] ? (operand_b_i[16] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0])\n                                               : (operand_b_i[16] ? operand_c_i[31:16] : operand_c_i[15:0]);\n          shuf_result[15:0]  = operand_b_i[1]  ? (operand_b_i[0] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0])\n                                               : (operand_b_i[0] ? operand_c_i[31:16] : operand_c_i[15:0]);\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_EXT: begin\n        if (mode8)      shuf_result = {24'h0, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8]};\n        else            shuf_result = {16'h0, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n      end\n      ALU_EXTS: begin\n        if (mode8)      shuf_result = {{24{operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i + 7]}}, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8]};\n        else            shuf_result = {{16{operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] + 15]}}, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n      end\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result = operand_c_i;\n          shuf_result[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8] = operand_a_i[7:0];\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result = imm_vec_ext_i[0] ? {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_c_i[15:0]}\n                                         : {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // bit counting\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [5:0] cnt_result;\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (.in_i(operand_a_i), .result_o(cnt_result));\n\n  wire [4:0] fl1_result = 5'd31 - ff1_result;\n\n  logic [5:0] bitop_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    bitop_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};\n      ALU_FL1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_result};\n      ALU_CNT: bitop_result = cnt_result;\n      ALU_CLB: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? (operand_a_i[31] ? 6'd31 : 6'd0) : clb_result;\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // divider (cycle-exact FSM; comparator from subtractor)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  wire         div_valid = enable_i & g_div;\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (shift_left_result),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(~|operand_a_i),\n      .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO(result_div),\n      .InVld_SI (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)\n  );\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // result mux (grouped OR)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] logic_result = (operator_i == ALU_AND) ? (operand_a_i & operand_b_i) :\n                             (operator_i == ALU_OR)  ? (operand_a_i | operand_b_i) :\n                                                       (operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i);\n\n  wire [31:0] cmp_fill = {{8{cmp_result[3]}}, {8{cmp_result[2]}}, {8{cmp_result[1]}}, {8{cmp_result[0]}}};\n  wire scalar_setless = g_cmp & operator_i[1] & ~operator_i[3];\n\n  wire [31:0] minmax_result = g_abs ? (is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]}\n                                                  : result_minmax)\n                                    : (g_clip ? clip_result : result_minmax);\n\n  wire g_rs_use = g_addfam | (g_shift & ~sll_op);\n\n  assign result_o = ({32{g_rs_use}} & rs_result)\n                  | ({32{g_logic}}  & logic_result)\n                  | ({32{sll_op}}   & shift_left_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bmanip}} & bmanip_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bitcnt}} & {26'h0, bitop_result})\n                  | ({32{g_extins | g_shuf}} & shuf_result)\n                  | ({32{g_cmp & ~scalar_setless}} & cmp_fill)\n                  | ({32{scalar_setless}} & {31'b0, cmp_result[3]})\n                  | ({32{g_minmax | g_abs | g_clip}} & minmax_result)\n                  | ({32{g_div}}    & result_div);\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- popcount ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- serial divider (cycle-exact; comparator from subtractor) ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  } State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n\n  // main adder. During DIVIDE PmSel_S is always 0, so the subtractor result\n  // is AReg - BReg: derive the upstream comparator from it.\n  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;\n  logic [C_WIDTH:0] SubOut_D;\n  assign SubOut_D = {1'b0, AddTmp_D} + {1'b0, ~AddMux_D} + {{(C_WIDTH-1){1'b0}}, 2'b01};\n  assign AddOut_D = PmSel_S ? (AddTmp_D + AddMux_D) : SubOut_D[C_WIDTH-1:0];\n  wire sub_ge  = SubOut_D[C_WIDTH];              // unsigned AReg >= BReg\n  wire sub_eq  = ~(|SubOut_D[C_WIDTH-1:0]);      // AReg == BReg\n  // upstream: (A==B) | ((A>B) ^ CompInv);  A>B = ge & ~eq\n  wire ab_cmp_raw = CompInv_SP ? (~sub_ge | sub_eq) : sub_ge;\n  assign ABComp_S = ab_cmp_raw & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n\n  assign Cnt_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBShift_DI : (~CntZero_S) ? Cnt_DP - 1 : Cnt_DP;\n  assign CntZero_S = ~(|Cnt_DP);\n\n  always_comb begin : p_fsm\n    State_SN   = State_SP;\n    OutVld_SO  = 1'b0;\n    LoadEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ARegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    BRegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ResRegEn_S = 1'b0;\n\n    case (State_SP)\n      IDLE: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (InVld_SI) begin\n          OutVld_SO = 1'b0;\n          ARegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          BRegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          LoadEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          State_SN  = DIVIDE;\n        end\n      end\n      DIVIDE: begin\n        ARegEn_S   = ABComp_S;\n        BRegEn_S   = 1'b1;\n        ResRegEn_S = 1'b1;\n        if (CntZero_S) begin\n          State_SN = FINISH;\n        end\n      end\n      FINISH: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (OutRdy_SI) begin\n          State_SN = IDLE;\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign RemSel_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? BMux_D : BReg_DP;\n  assign ResReg_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? '0 : (ResRegEn_S) ? {\n    ABComp_S, ResReg_DP[$high(ResReg_DP):1]\n  } : ResReg_DP;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge Clk_CI or negedge Rst_RBI) begin : p_regs\n    if (~Rst_RBI) begin\n      State_SP   <= IDLE;\n      AReg_DP    <= '0;\n      BReg_DP    <= '0;\n      ResReg_DP  <= '0;\n      Cnt_DP     <= '0;\n      RemSel_SP  <= 1'b0;\n      CompInv_SP <= 1'b0;\n      ResInv_SP  <= 1'b0;\n    end else begin\n      State_SP   <= State_SN;\n      AReg_DP    <= AReg_DN;\n      BReg_DP    <= BReg_DN;\n      ResReg_DP  <= ResReg_DN;\n      Cnt_DP     <= Cnt_DN;\n      RemSel_SP  <= RemSel_SN;\n      CompInv_SP <= CompInv_SN;\n      ResInv_SP  <= ResInv_SN;\n    end\n  end\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design6.sv')

operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[7:0]   = operand_b_i[2]  ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0] +: 8];\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result[31:16] = operand_b_i[17] ? (operand_b_i[16] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0])\n                                               : (operand_b_i[16] ? operand_c_i[31:16] : operand_c_i[15:0]);\n          shuf_result[15:0]  = operand_b_i[1]  ? (operand_b_i[0] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0])\n                                               : (operand_b_i[0] ? operand_c_i[31:16] : operand_c_i[15:0]);\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_EXT: begin\n        if (mode8)      shuf_result = {24'h0, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8]};\n        else            shuf_result = {16'h0, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n      end\n      ALU_EXTS: begin\n        if (mode8)      shuf_result = {{24{operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i + 7]}}, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8]};\n        else            shuf_result = {{16{operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] + 15]}}, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n      end\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result = operand_c_i;\n          shuf_result[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8] = operand_a_i[7:0];\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result = imm_vec_ext_i[0] ? {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_c_i[15:0]}\n                                         : {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // bit counting\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [5:0] cnt_result;\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (.in_i(operand_a_i), .result_o(cnt_result));\n\n  wire [4:0] fl1_result = 5'd31 - ff1_result;\n\n  logic [5:0] bitop_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    bitop_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};\n      ALU_FL1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_result};\n      ALU_CNT: bitop_result = cnt_result;\n      ALU_CLB: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? (operand_a_i[31] ? 6'd31 : 6'd0) : clb_result;\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // divider (cycle-exact FSM; comparator from subtractor)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  wire         div_valid = enable_i & g_div;\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (shift_left_result),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(~|operand_a_i),\n      .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO(result_div),\n      .InVld_SI (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)\n  );\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // result mux (grouped OR)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] logic_result = (operator_i == ALU_AND) ? (operand_a_i & operand_b_i) :\n                             (operator_i == ALU_OR)  ? (operand_a_i | operand_b_i) :\n                                                       (operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i);\n\n  wire [31:0] cmp_fill = {{8{cmp_result[3]}}, {8{cmp_result[2]}}, {8{cmp_result[1]}}, {8{cmp_result[0]}}};\n  wire scalar_setless = g_cmp & operator_i[1] & ~operator_i[3];\n\n  wire [31:0] minmax_result = g_abs ? (is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]}\n                                                  : result_minmax)\n                                    : (g_clip ? clip_result : result_minmax);\n\n  wire g_rs_b_use = g_shift & ~sll_op;   // SRA SRL ROR\n\n  assign result_o = ({32{g_logic}}   & logic_result)\n                  | ({32{g_addfam}}  & rs_a_result)\n                  | ({32{g_rs_b_use}} & rs_b_result)\n                  | ({32{sll_op}}    & shift_left_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bmanip}}  & bmanip_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bitcnt}}  & {26'h0, bitop_result})\n                  | ({32{g_extins | g_shuf}} & shuf_result)\n                  | ({32{g_cmp & ~scalar_setless}} & cmp_fill)\n                  | ({32{scalar_setless}} & {31'b0, cmp_result[3]})\n                  | ({32{g_minmax | g_abs | g_clip}} & minmax_result)\n                  | ({32{g_div}}     & result_div);\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- popcount ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n    for (n = 0; comparator from subtractor) ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  } State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n\n  // main adder. During DIVIDE PmSel_S is 0, so SubOut_D is AReg - BReg:\n  // derive the upstream comparator ((A==B)|((A>B)^CompInv)) from it.\n  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;\n  logic [C_WIDTH:0] SubOut_D;\n  assign SubOut_D = {1'b0, AddTmp_D} + {1'b0, ~AddMux_D} + {{(C_WIDTH-1){1'b0}}, 2'b01};\n  assign AddOut_D = PmSel_S ? (AddTmp_D + AddMux_D) : SubOut_D[C_WIDTH-1:0];\n  wire sub_ge  = SubOut_D[C_WIDTH];              // unsigned AReg >= BReg\n  wire sub_eq  = ~(|SubOut_D[C_WIDTH-1:0]);      // AReg == BReg\n  // A>B = ge & ~eq;  upstream = eq | (gt ^ CompInv):\n  //   CompInv=0 -> eq | gt = ge ;  CompInv=1 -> eq | ~gt = ~ge | eq\n  wire ab_cmp_raw = CompInv_SP ? (~sub_ge | sub_eq) : sub_ge;\n  assign ABComp_S = ab_cmp_raw & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n\n  assign Cnt_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBShift_DI : (~CntZero_S) ? Cnt_DP - 1 : Cnt_DP;\n  assign CntZero_S = ~(|Cnt_DP);\n\n  always_comb begin : p_fsm\n    State_SN   = State_SP;\n    OutVld_SO  = 1'b0;\n    LoadEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ARegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    BRegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ResRegEn_S = 1'b0;\n\n    case (State_SP)\n      IDLE: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (InVld_SI) begin\n          OutVld_SO = 1'b0;\n          ARegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          BRegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          LoadEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          State_SN  = DIVIDE;\n        end\n      end\n      DIVIDE: begin\n        ARegEn_S   = ABComp_S;\n        BRegEn_S   = 1'b1;\n        ResRegEn_S = 1'b1;\n        if (CntZero_S) begin\n          State_SN = FINISH;\n        end\n      end\n      FINISH: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (OutRdy_SI) begin\n          State_SN = IDLE;\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign RemSel_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? '0 : (ResRegEn_S) ? {\n    ABComp_S, ResReg_DP[$high(ResReg_DP):1]\n  } : ResReg_DP;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge Clk_CI or negedge Rst_RBI) begin : p_regs\n    if (~Rst_RBI) begin\n      State_SP   <= IDLE;\n      AReg_DP    <= '0;\n      BReg_DP    <= '0;\n      ResReg_DP  <= '0;\n      Cnt_DP     <= '0;\n      RemSel_SP  <= 1'b0;\n      CompInv_SP <= 1'b0;\n      ResInv_SP  <= 1'b0;\n    end else begin\n      State_SP   <= State_SN;\n      AReg_DP    <= AReg_DN;\n      BReg_DP    <= BReg_DN;\n      ResReg_DP  <= ResReg_DN;\n      Cnt_DP     <= Cnt_DN;\n      RemSel_SP  <= RemSel_SN;\n      CompInv_SP <= CompInv_SN;\n      ResInv_SP  <= ResInv_SN;\n    end\n  end\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design7.sv')

----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire mode32 = (vector_mode_i == 2'b00);\n  wire mode16 = (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE16);\n  wire mode8  = (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8);\n\n  wire g_addfam = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0011);\n  wire g_shift  = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01001);         // SRA SRL ROR SLL\n  wire g_div    = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01100);         // DIVU DIV REMU REM\n  wire g_bitcnt = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01101);         // CNT CLB FF1 FL1\n  wire g_brev   = (operator_i == ALU_BREV);\n  wire g_bmanip = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01010) ||       // BEXT BEXTU BINS BCLR\n                  (operator_i == ALU_BSET) || g_brev;\n  wire g_bext   = (operator_i == ALU_BEXT);\n  wire g_bins   = (operator_i == ALU_BINS);\n  wire g_cmp    = (operator_i[6:4] == 3'b000);\n  wire g_minmax = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00100);\n  wire g_abs    = (operator_i == ALU_ABS);\n  wire g_clip   = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP) || (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU);\n  wire g_shuf   = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01110);\n  wire g_extins = (operator_i == ALU_EXT) || (operator_i == ALU_EXTS) || (operator_i == ALU_INS);\n  wire g_logic  = (operator_i == ALU_AND) || (operator_i == ALU_OR) || (operator_i == ALU_XOR);\n\n  wire sll_op = (operator_i == ALU_SLL);\n  wire sra_op = (operator_i == ALU_SRA);\n  wire ror_op = (operator_i == ALU_ROR);\n\n  // left-shift users of the shared barrel (data/amount bit-reversed)\n  wire shift_left = sll_op | g_bins | g_brev | g_div;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // segmented adder (adder family, ABS, CLIP)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire addfam_sub = g_addfam & operator_i[0];\n  wire b_invert   = addfam_sub | is_subrot_i;\n  wire abs_op     = g_abs;\n  wire clip_op    = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP);\n  wire neg        = b_invert | abs_op | clip_op;\n\n  wire [31:0] add_op_a = abs_op ? ~operand_a_i :\n   // meaningful within g_addfam\n  wire [31:0] round_sum = adder_result + (addfam_round ? {1'b0, bmask[31:1]} : 32'h0);\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // RS_A: adder-family per-lane right shifter (data = round_sum, amt = fam_sh)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire addfam_arith = ~operator_i[1];\n  wire [4:0] fam_sh32 = is_clpx_i ? {3'b000, clpx_shift_i} : bmask_b_i;\n  wire [3:0] fam_sh16 = is_clpx_i ? {2'b00, clpx_shift_i}  : bmask_b_i[3:0];\n  wire [2:0] fam_sh8  = is_clpx_i ? {1'b0, clpx_shift_i}   : bmask_b_i[2:0];\n\n  logic [31:0] rs_a_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (mode16) begin\n      rs_a_result[31:16] = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[31], round_sum[31:16]}) >>> fam_sh16;\n      rs_a_result[15:0]  = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[15], round_sum[15:0]})  >>> fam_sh16;\n    end else if (mode8) begin\n      rs_a_result[31:24] = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[31], round_sum[31:24]}) >>> fam_sh8;\n      rs_a_result[23:16] = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[23], round_sum[23:16]}) >>> fam_sh8;\n      rs_a_result[15:8]  = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[15], round_sum[15:8]})  >>> fam_sh8;\n      rs_a_result[7:0]   = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[7],  round_sum[7:0]})   >>> fam_sh8;\n    end else begin\n      rs_a_result = $signed({{32{addfam_arith & round_sum[31]}}, round_sum}) >>> fam_sh32;\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // ff1 machinery (bit-count ops + div_shift) -- needed before shared barrel\n  // (upstream uses ff/cnt for div_shift and the div input operand)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] operand_a_rev;\n  genvar ri;\n  generate\n    for (ri = 0; ri < 32; ri++) begin : gen_a_rev\n      assign operand_a_rev[ri] = operand_a_i[31-ri];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire ff1_op = (operator_i == ALU_FF1);\n  wire sign_flip = (g_div & operator_i[0]) | (operator_i == ALU_CLB);\n  wire [31:0] ff_input = ff1_op ? operand_a_i : (operand_a_rev ^ {32{sign_flip & operand_a_i[31]}});\n  wire [31:0] ff_onehot = ff_input & (~ff_input + 32'd1);\n  wire        ff_no_one = ~|ff_input;\n  logic [4:0] ff1_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    ff1_result = 5'd0;\n    for (int i = 1; i < 32; i++)\n      if (ff_onehot[i]) ff1_result = i[4:0];\n  end\n  wire [5:0] clb_result = ff1_result - 5'd1;\n\n  wire         div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & operator_i[0];\n  wire [5:0]   div_shift_int   = ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result;\n  wire [5:0]   div_shift       = div_shift_int + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // shared barrel (upstream-style): right shift; left shifts via reversal\n  //   data:  shift_left ? operand_a_rev : operand_a\n  //   arith: SRA | BEXT (right ops only)\n  //   amt:   shift_left ? div_shift/b_rev : operand_b lanes\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire rs_b_arith = sra_op | g_bext;\n  wire ror32 = ror_op & mode32;\n  wire [31:0] sh_data32 = shift_left ? operand_a_rev : operand_a_i;\n  wire [63:0] sh_in = ror32 ? {operand_a_i, operand_a_i} :\n                              {{32{rs_b_arith & sh_data32[31]}}, sh_data32};\n\n  // shift amount (reversed lanes for left shifts)\n  logic [31:0] sh_amt;\n  wire [31:0] sh_amt_base = g_div ? {26'b0, div_shift[4:0]} : operand_b_i;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (shift_left) begin\n      case (vector_mode_i)\n        VEC_MODE16: sh_amt = {sh_amt_base[15:0], sh_amt_base[31:16]};\n        VEC_MODE8:  sh_amt = {sh_amt_base[7:0], sh_amt_base[15:8], sh_amt_base[23:16], sh_amt_base[31:24]};\n        default:    sh_amt = sh_amt_base;\n      endcase\n    end else sh_amt = sh_amt_base;\n  end\n\n  logic [31:0] sh_result_r;   // right-shift result\n  always_comb begin\n    if (mode16 & ~ror32) begin\n      sh_result_r[31:16] = $signed({rs_b_arith & sh_data32[31], sh_data32[31:16]}) >>> sh_amt[19:16];\n      sh_result_r[15:0]  = $signed({rs_b_arith & sh_data32[15], sh_data32[15:0]})  >>> sh_amt[3:0];\n    end else if (mode8 & ~ror32) begin\n      sh_result_r[31:24] = $signed({rs_b_arith & sh_data32[31], sh_data32[31:24]}) >>> sh_amt[26:24];\n      sh_result_r[23:16] = $signed({rs_b_arith & sh_data32[23], sh_data32[23:16]}) >>> sh_amt[18:16];\n      sh_result_r[15:8]  = $signed({rs_b_arith & sh_data32[15], sh_data32[15:8]})  >>> sh_amt[10:8];\n      sh_result_r[7:0]   = $signed({rs_b_arith & sh_data32[7],  sh_data32[7:0]})   >>> sh_amt[2:0];\n    end else begin\n      sh_result_r = sh_in >> sh_amt[4:0];\n    end\n  end\n\n  // bit-reversed output = left-shift result (SLL/BINS/BREV/div)\n  wire [31:0] shift_left_result;\n  generate\n    for (ri = 0; bj < 32; bj++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[bj] = shift_result[31-bj];\n    end\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 16; bj++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*bj+1:2*bj] = shift_result[31-bj*2:31-bj*2-1];\n    end\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 10; bj++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*bj+2:3*bj] = shift_result[31-bj*3:31-bj*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n  always_comb begin\n    unique case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n      2'b01:   brev_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10:   brev_result = radix_8_rev;\n      default: brev_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  logic [31:0] bmanip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (operator_i == ALU_BCLR)       bmanip_result = bclr_result;\n    else if (operator_i == ALU_BSET)  bmanip_result = bset_result;\n    else if (g_brev)                  bmanip_result = brev_result;\n    else                              bmanip_result = bextins_result;\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // comparisons + min/max/abs/clip\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [3:0] cmp_signed;\n  wire [3:0] is_equal_vec;\n  wire [3:0] is_greater_vec;\n\n  wire cmp_grp_signed = g_cmp & ~operator_i[0];\n  wire minmax_signed  = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MAX);\n  wire signed_family  = cmp_grp_signed | minmax_signed | g_abs | g_clip;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    if (signed_family) begin\n      if (mode8)       cmp_signed = 4'b1111;\n      else if (mode16) cmp_signed = 4'b1010;\n      else             cmp_signed = 4'b1000;\n    end else cmp_signed = 4'b0000;\n  end\n\n  genvar ci;\n  generate\n    for (ci = 0; (operand_a_i | operand_b_i) :\n                                                       (operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i);\n\n  wire [31:0] cmp_fill = {{8{cmp_result[3]}}, {8{cmp_result[2]}}, {8{cmp_result[1]}}, {8{cmp_result[0]}}};\n  wire scalar_setless = g_cmp & operator_i[1] & ~operator_i[3];\n\n  wire [31:0] minmax_result = g_abs ?   // uses shift_result\n\n  assign result_o = ({32{g_logic}}   & logic_result)\n                  | ({32{g_addfam}}  & rs_a_result)\n                  | ({32{g_rs_b_use}} & shift_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bitcnt}}  & {26'h0, bitop_result})\n                  | ({32{g_extins | g_shuf}} & shuf_result)\n                  | ({32{g_cmp & ~scalar_setless}} & cmp_fill)\n                  | ({32{scalar_setless}} & {31'b0, cmp_result[3]})\n                  | ({32{g_minmax | g_abs | g_clip}} & minmax_result)\n                  | ({32{g_div}}     & result_div);\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- popcount ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- serial divider (cycle-exact; comparator from subtractor) ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  } State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n\n  // main adder; during DIVIDE PmSel_S is 0 so SubOut_D is AReg - BReg.\n  // Derive the upstream comparator (eq | (gt ^ CompInv)) from it.\n  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;\n  logic [C_WIDTH:0] SubOut_D;\n  assign SubOut_D = {1'b0, AddTmp_D} + {1'b0, ~AddMux_D} + {{(C_WIDTH-1){1'b0}}, 2'b01};\n  assign AddOut_D = PmSel_S ? (AddTmp_D + AddMux_D) : SubOut_D[C_WIDTH-1:0];\n  wire sub_ge  = SubOut_D[C_WIDTH];\n  wire sub_eq  = ~(|SubOut_D[C_WIDTH-1:0]);\n  wire ab_cmp_raw = CompInv_SP ? (~sub_ge | sub_eq) : sub_ge;\n  assign ABComp_S = ab_cmp_raw & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n\n  assign Cnt_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBShift_DI : (~CntZero_S) ? Cnt_DP - 1 : Cnt_DP;\n  assign CntZero_S = ~(|Cnt_DP);\n\n  always_comb begin : p_fsm\n    State_SN   = State_SP;\n    OutVld_SO  = 1'b0;\n    LoadEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ARegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    BRegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ResRegEn_S = 1'b0;\n\n    case (State_SP)\n      IDLE: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (InVld_SI) begin\n          OutVld_SO = 1'b0;\n          ARegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          BRegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          LoadEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          State_SN  = DIVIDE;\n        end\n      end\n      DIVIDE: begin\n        ARegEn_S   = ABComp_S;\n        BRegEn_S   = 1'b1;\n        ResRegEn_S = 1'b1;\n        if (CntZero_S) begin\n          State_SN = FINISH;\n        end\n      end\n      FINISH: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (OutRdy_SI) begin\n          State_SN = IDLE;\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign RemSel_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpCode_SI[1] : RemSel_SP;\n  assign CompInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBSign_SI : CompInv_SP;\n  assign ResInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? (~OpBIsZero_SI | OpCode_SI[1]) & OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(\n      OpA_DI\n  )] ^ OpBSign_SI) : ResInv_SP;\n\n  assign AReg_DN = (ARegEn_S) ? AddOut_D : AReg_DP;\n  assign BReg_DN = (BRegEn_S) ? BMux_D : BReg_DP;\n  assign ResReg_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? '0 : (ResRegEn_S) ? {\n    ABComp_S, ResReg_DP[$high(ResReg_DP):1]\n  } : ResReg_DP;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge Clk_CI or negedge Rst_RBI) begin : p_regs\n    if (~Rst_RBI) begin\n      State_SP   <= IDLE;\n      AReg_DP    <= '0;\n      BReg_DP    <= '0;\n      ResReg_DP  <= '0;\n      Cnt_DP     <= '0;\n      RemSel_SP  <= 1'b0;\n      CompInv_SP <= 1'b0;\n      ResInv_SP  <= 1'b0;\n    end else begin\n      State_SP   <= State_SN;\n      AReg_DP    <= AReg_DN;\n      BReg_DP    <= BReg_DN;\n      ResReg_DP  <= ResReg_DN;\n      Cnt_DP     <= Cnt_DN;\n      RemSel_SP  <= RemSel_SN;\n      CompInv_SP <= CompInv_SN;\n      ResInv_SP  <= ResInv_SN;\n    end\n  end\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design8.sv')

(operand_b_i[0] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0])\n                                               : (operand_b_i[0] ? operand_c_i[31:16] : operand_c_i[15:0]);\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_EXT: begin\n        if (mode8)      shuf_result = {24'h0, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8]};\n        else            shuf_result = {16'h0, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n      end\n      ALU_EXTS: begin\n        if (mode8)      shuf_result = {{24{operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i + 7]}}, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8]};\n        else            shuf_result = {{16{operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] + 15]}}, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n      end\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result = operand_c_i;\n          shuf_result[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8] = operand_a_i[7:0];\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result = imm_vec_ext_i[0] ? {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_c_i[15:0]}\n                                         : {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // bit counting\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [5:0] cnt_result;\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (.in_i(operand_a_i), .result_o(cnt_result));\n\n  wire [4:0] fl1_result = 5'd31 - ff1_result;\n\n  logic [5:0] bitop_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    bitop_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};\n      ALU_FL1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_result};\n      ALU_CNT: bitop_result = cnt_result;\n      ALU_CLB: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? (operand_a_i | operand_b_i) :\n                                                       (operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i);\n\n  wire [31:0] cmp_fill = {{8{cmp_result[3]}}, {8{cmp_result[2]}}, {8{cmp_result[1]}}, {8{cmp_result[0]}}};\n  wire scalar_setless = g_cmp & operator_i[1] & ~operator_i[3];\n\n  wire [31:0] minmax_result = g_abs ? (is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]}\n                                                  : result_minmax)\n                                    : (g_clip ? clip_result : result_minmax);\n\n  wire g_rs_b_use = (g_shift & ~sll_op);   // SRA SRL ROR\n\n  assign result_o = ({32{g_logic}}   & logic_result)\n                  | ({32{g_addfam}}  & rs_a_result)\n                  | ({32{g_rs_b_use}} & shift_result)\n                  | ({32{sll_op}}    & shift_left_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bmanip}}  & bmanip_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bitcnt}}  & {26'h0, bitop_result})\n                  | ({32{g_extins | g_shuf}} & shuf_result)\n                  | ({32{g_cmp & ~scalar_setless}} & cmp_fill)\n                  | ({32{scalar_setless}} & {31'b0, cmp_result[3]})\n                  | ({32{g_minmax | g_abs | g_clip}} & minmax_result)\n                  | ({32{g_div}}     & result_div);\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- popcount ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- Kogge-Stone subtractor: {ge, diff} = a - b (N-bit -> N+1 bits)\nmodule ks_subn #(\n    parameter int N = 32\n) (\n    input  logic [N-1:0] a,\n    input  logic [N-1:0] b,\n    output logic         ge,     // unsigned a >= b (carry out)\n    output logic [N-1:0] diff\n);\n  localparam int L = $clog2(N);\n  wire [N-1:0] Ax = a;\n  wire [N-1:0] Bx = ~b;   // a + ~b + 1\n\n  logic [L:0][N-1:0] G, P;\n\n  assign G[0] = Ax & Bx;\n  assign P[0] = Ax ^ Bx;\n\n  genvar lvl, i;\n  generate\n    for (lvl = 1; lvl <= L; lvl++) begin : gen_lvl\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : gen_bit\n        if (i >= (1 << (lvl-1))) begin : gen_comb\n          assign G[lvl][i] = G[lvl-1][i] | (P[lvl-1][i] & G[lvl-1][i - (1<<(lvl-1))]);\n          assign P[lvl][i] = P[lvl-1][i] & P[lvl-1][i - (1<<(lvl-1))];\n        end else begin : gen_pass\n          assign G[lvl][i] = G[lvl-1][i];\n          assign P[lvl][i] = P[lvl-1][i];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // cin = 1 folded into bit 0: carry_in[0] = 1; carry_in[i>0] = G[L][i-1]\n  assign diff = P[0] ^ {G[L][N-2:0], 1'b1};\n  // carry out of bit N-1 = G[L][N-1], with cin folded: g0' = g0 | p0\n  assign ge = G[L][N-1] | P[L][N-1];\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- serial divider (cycle-exact FSM; KS subtractor) ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  } State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n\n  // Single KS subtractor, input-muxed:\n  //   DIVIDE: AReg - BReg  (loop subtract)\n  //   LOAD:   0 - OpA      (negation for load path; +OpA handled by mux)\n  wire [C_WIDTH-1:0] sub_a = LoadEn_S ? {C_WIDTH{1'b0}} : AReg_DP;\n  wire [C_WIDTH-1:0] sub_b = LoadEn_S ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n  wire               sub_ge;\n  wire [C_WIDTH-1:0] sub_diff;\n  ks_subn #(.N(C_WIDTH)) ks_i (.a(sub_a), .b(sub_b), .ge(sub_ge), .diff(sub_diff));\n\n  wire [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D = (LoadEn_S & PmSel_S) ? OpA_DI : sub_diff;\n  // comparator (only sampled during DIVIDE): upstream = eq | (gt ^ CompInv)\n  wire sub_eq = ~(|sub_diff);\n  wire ab_cmp_raw = CompInv_SP ? (~sub_ge | sub_eq) : sub_ge;\n  assign ABComp_S = ab_cmp_raw & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n\n  assign Cnt_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBShift_DI : (~CntZero_S) ? Cnt_DP - 1 : Cnt_DP;\n  assign CntZero_S = ~(|Cnt_DP);\n\n  always_comb begin : p_fsm\n    State_SN   = State_SP;\n    OutVld_SO  = 1'b0;\n    LoadEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ARegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    BRegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ResRegEn_S = 1'b0;\n\n    case (State_SP)\n      IDLE: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (InVld_SI) begin\n          OutVld_SO = 1'b0;\n          ARegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          BRegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          LoadEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          State_SN  = DIVIDE;\n        end\n      end\n      DIVIDE: begin\n        ARegEn_S   = ABComp_S;\n        BRegEn_S   = 1'b1;\n        ResRegEn_S = 1'b1;\n        if (CntZero_S) begin\n          State_SN = FINISH;\n        end\n      end\n      FINISH: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (OutRdy_SI) begin\n          State_SN = IDLE;\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign RemSel_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLB   = 7'b0110101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EXTS  = 7'b0111110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EXT   = 7'b0111111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ABS   = 7'b0010100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLIP  = 7'b0010110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLIPU = 7'b0010111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_INS   = 7'b0101101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MIN   = 7'b0010000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MINU  = 7'b0010001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MAX   = 7'b0010010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MAXU  = 7'b0010011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SHUF  = 7'b0111010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SHUF2 = 7'b0111011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_PCKLO = 7'b0111000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_PCKHI = 7'b0111001;\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE16 = 2'b10;\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE8  = 2'b11;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // opcode group decodes\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire mode32 = (vector_mode_i == 2'b00);\n  wire mode16 = (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE16);\n  wire mode8  = (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8);\n\n  wire g_addfam = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0011);\n  wire g_shift  = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01001);         // SRA SRL ROR SLL\n  wire g_div    = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01100);         // DIVU DIV REMU REM\n  wire g_bitcnt = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01101);         // CNT CLB FF1 FL1\n  wire g_brev   = (operator_i == ALU_BREV);\n  wire g_bmanip = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01010) ||       // BEXT BEXTU BINS BCLR\n                  (operator_i == ALU_BSET) || g_brev;\n  wire g_bext   = (operator_i == ALU_BEXT);\n  wire g_bins   = (operator_i == ALU_BINS);\n  wire g_cmp    = (operator_i[6:4] == 3'b000);\n  wire g_minmax = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00100);\n  wire g_abs    = (operator_i == ALU_ABS);\n  wire g_clip   = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP) || (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU);\n  wire g_shuf   = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01110);\n  wire g_extins = (operator_i == ALU_EXT) || (operator_i == ALU_EXTS) || (operator_i == ALU_INS);\n  wire g_logic  = (operator_i == ALU_AND) || (operator_i == ALU_OR) || (operator_i == ALU_XOR);\n\n  wire sll_op = (operator_i == ALU_SLL);\n  wire sra_op = (operator_i == ALU_SRA);\n  wire ror_op = (operator_i == ALU_ROR);\n\n  // left-barrel users: SLL, BINS, BREV, div, and ROR's 32-bit left half\n  wire shift_left = sll_op | g_bins | g_brev | g_div | (ror_op & mode32);\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // segmented adder (adder family, ABS, CLIP)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire addfam_sub = g_addfam & operator_i[0];\n  wire b_invert   = addfam_sub | is_subrot_i;\n  wire abs_op     = g_abs;\n  wire clip_op    = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP);\n  wire neg        = b_invert | abs_op | clip_op;\n\n  wire [31:0] add_op_a = abs_op ? ~operand_a_i :\n                         is_subrot_i ? {operand_b_i[15:0], operand_a_i[31:16]} : operand_a_i;\n  wire [31:0] add_op_b = b_invert ? (is_subrot_i ? ~{operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[31:16]}\n                                                  : ~operand_b_i)\n                                   : operand_b_i;\n\n  wire sep9_a  = neg | ~mode8;\n  wire sep18_a = neg | mode32;\n  wire sep27_a = neg | ~mode8;\n  wire sep0_b  = neg;\n  wire sep9_b  = neg & mode8;\n  wire sep18_b = neg & (mode8 | mode16);\n  wire sep27_b = neg & mode8;\n\n  wire [35:0] adder_in_a = {add_op_a[31:24], sep27_a,\n                            add_op_a[23:16], sep18_a,\n                            add_op_a[15:8],  sep9_a,\n                            add_op_a[7:0],   1'b1};\n  wire [35:0] adder_in_b = {add_op_b[31:24], sep27_b,\n                            add_op_b[23:16], sep18_b,\n                            add_op_b[15:8],  sep9_b,\n                            add_op_b[7:0],   sep0_b};\n\n  wire [36:0] adder_result_expanded = $signed(adder_in_a) + $signed(adder_in_b);\n  wire [31:0] adder_result = {adder_result_expanded[35:28],\n                              adder_result_expanded[26:19],\n                              adder_result_expanded[17:10],\n                              adder_result_expanded[8:1]};\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // rounding add (adder family)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] bmask = (~((32'hFFFFFFFE) << bmask_a_i)) << bmask_b_i;\n  wire addfam_round = operator_i[2];   // meaningful within g_addfam\n  wire [31:0] round_sum = adder_result + (addfam_round ? {1'b0, bmask[31:1]} : 32'h0);\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // RS_A: adder-family per-lane right shifter\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire addfam_arith = ~operator_i[1];\n  wire [4:0] fam_sh32 = is_clpx_i ? {3'b000, clpx_shift_i} : bmask_b_i;\n  wire [3:0] fam_sh16 = is_clpx_i ? {2'b00, clpx_shift_i}  : bmask_b_i[3:0];\n  wire [2:0] fam_sh8  = is_clpx_i ? {1'b0, clpx_shift_i}   : bmask_b_i[2:0];\n\n  logic [31:0] rs_a_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (mode16) begin\n      rs_a_result[31:16] = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[31], round_sum[31:16]}) >>> fam_sh16;\n      rs_a_result[15:0]  = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[15], round_sum[15:0]})  >>> fam_sh16;\n    end else if (mode8) begin\n      rs_a_result[31:24] = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[31], round_sum[31:24]}) >>> fam_sh8;\n      rs_a_result[23:16] = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[23], round_sum[23:16]}) >>> fam_sh8;\n      rs_a_result[15:8]  = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[15], round_sum[15:8]})  >>> fam_sh8;\n      rs_a_result[7:0]   = $signed({addfam_arith & round_sum[7],  round_sum[7:0]})   >>> fam_sh8;\n    end else begin\n      rs_a_result = $signed({{32{addfam_arith & round_sum[31]}}, round_sum}) >>> fam_sh32;\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // ff1 machinery (bit-count ops + div_shift)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] operand_a_rev;\n  genvar ri;\n  generate\n    for (ri = 0; ri < 32; ri++) begin : gen_a_rev\n      assign operand_a_rev[ri] = operand_a_i[31-ri];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire ff1_op = (operator_i == ALU_FF1);\n  wire sign_flip = (g_div & operator_i[0]) | (operator_i == ALU_CLB);\n  wire [31:0] ff_input = ff1_op ? operand_a_i : (operand_a_rev ^ {32{sign_flip & operand_a_i[31]}});\n  wire [31:0] ff_onehot = ff_input & (~ff_input + 32'd1);\n  wire        ff_no_one = ~|ff_input;\n  logic [4:0] ff1_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    ff1_result = 5'd0;\n    for (int i = 1; i < 32; i++)\n      if (ff_onehot[i]) ff1_result = i[4:0];\n  end\n  wire [5:0] clb_result = ff1_result - 5'd1;\n\n  wire         div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & operator_i[0];\n  wire [5:0]   div_shift_int   = ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result;\n  wire [5:0]   div_shift       = div_shift_int + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // right barrel (32-bit ops only; per-lane via sized sub-expressions)\n  //   users: SRA/SRL (per lane), ROR-right (mode32) / ROR=per-lane SRL (vec),\n  //          BEXT/BEXTU (32-bit)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire rs_b_arith = sra_op | g_bext;\n\n  logic [31:0] sh_result_r;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (mode16 & ~ror_op) begin\n      sh_result_r[31:16] = $signed({rs_b_arith & operand_a_i[31], operand_a_i[31:16]}) >>> operand_b_i[19:16];\n      sh_result_r[15:0]  = $signed({rs_b_arith & operand_a_i[15], operand_a_i[15:0]})  >>> operand_b_i[3:0];\n    end else if (mode8 & ~ror_op) begin\n      sh_result_r[31:24] = $signed({rs_b_arith & operand_a_i[31], operand_a_i[31:24]}) >>> operand_b_i[26:24];\n      sh_result_r[23:16] = $signed({rs_b_arith & operand_a_i[23], operand_a_i[23:16]}) >>> operand_b_i[18:16];\n      sh_result_r[15:8]  = $signed({rs_b_arith & operand_a_i[15], operand_a_i[15:8]})  >>> operand_b_i[10:8];\n      sh_result_r[7:0]   = $signed({rs_b_arith & operand_a_i[7],  operand_a_i[7:0]})   >>> operand_b_i[2:0];\n    end else begin\n      // 32-bit mode (SRA/SRL/BEXT/BEXTU) or ROR right half (mode32):\n      sh_result_r = rs_b_arith ? ($signed(operand_a_i) >>> operand_b_i[4:0])\n                               : (operand_a_i >> operand_b_i[4:0]);\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // left barrel via bit-reversal (right shift of reversed data)\n  //   users: SLL (per lane), BINS/BREV/div (32-bit), ROR left half (mode32)\n  //   amount for ROR left half: 32 - b[4:0] (mod 32)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] ls_amt_rev;\n  wire [5:0] ls_amt32_raw = (ror_op & mode32) ? (6'd32 - {1'b0, operand_b_i[4:0]})\n                                              : (g_div ? div_shift : {1'b0, operand_b_i[4:0]});\n  wire [31:0] ls_amt_base = (g_div | (ror_op & mode32)) ? {26'b0, ls_amt32_raw[4:0]} : operand_b_i;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (shift_left) begin\n      case (vector_mode_i)\n        VEC_MODE16: ls_amt_rev = {ls_amt_base[15:0], ls_amt_base[31:16]};\n        VEC_MODE8:  ls_amt_rev = {ls_amt_base[7:0], ls_amt_base[15:8], ls_amt_base[23:16], ls_amt_base[31:24]};\n        default:    ls_amt_rev = ls_amt_base;\n      endcase\n    end else ls_amt_rev = ls_amt_base;\n  end\n\n  logic [31:0] rs_l_out;   // right-shift of reversed data\n  always_comb begin\n    if (mode16 & sll_op) begin\n      rs_l_out[31:16] = operand_a_rev[31:16] >> ls_amt_rev[19:16];\n      rs_l_out[15:0]  = operand_a_rev[15:0]  >> ls_amt_rev[3:0];\n    end else if (mode8 & sll_op) begin\n      rs_l_out[31:24] = operand_a_rev[31:24] >> ls_amt_rev[26:24];\n      rs_l_out[23:16] = operand_a_rev[23:16] >> ls_amt_rev[18:16];\n      rs_l_out[15:8]  = operand_a_rev[15:8]  >> ls_amt_rev[10:8];\n      rs_l_out[7:0]   = operand_a_rev[7:0]   >> ls_amt_rev[2:0];\n    end else begin\n      rs_l_out = operand_a_rev >> ls_amt_rev[4:0];\n    end\n  end\n\n  wire [31:0] shift_left_result;\n  generate\n    for (ri = 0; ri < 32; ri++) begin : gen_left_result\n      assign shift_left_result[ri] = rs_l_out[31-ri];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // final shift result for shift ops (ROR combines both barrels in mode32)\n  wire [31:0] shift_result = (ror_op & mode32) ? (sh_result_r | shift_left_result)\n                                               : (shift_left ? shift_left_result : sh_result_r);\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // bit manipulation (32-bit mode)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire        extract_sign   = g_bext & sh_result_r[bmask_a_i];\n  wire [31:0] bextins_and    = g_bins ? operand_c_i : {32{extract_sign}};\n  wire [31:0] bextins_result = (bmask & (g_bins ? shift_left_result : sh_result_r))\n                             | (bextins_and & ~bmask);\n  wire [31:0] bclr_result    = operand_a_i & ~bmask;\n  wire [31:0] bset_result    = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n  logic [31:0] radix_2_rev, radix_4_rev, radix_8_rev, brev_result;\n  genvar bj;\n  generate\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 32; bj++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[bj] = shift_left_result[31-bj];\n    end\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 16; bj++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*bj+1:2*bj] = shift_left_result[31-bj*2:31-bj*2-1];\n    end\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 10; bj++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*bj+2:3*bj] = shift_left_result[31-bj*3:31-bj*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n  always_comb begin\n    unique case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n      2'b01:   brev_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10:   brev_result = radix_8_rev;\n      default: brev_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  logic [31:0] bmanip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (operator_i == ALU_BCLR)       bmanip_result = bclr_result;\n    else if (operator_i == ALU_BSET)  bmanip_result = bset_result;\n    else if (g_brev)                  bmanip_result = brev_result;\n    else                              bmanip_result = bextins_result;\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // comparisons + min/max/abs/clip\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [3:0] cmp_signed;\n  wire [3:0] is_equal_vec;\n  wire [3:0] is_greater_vec;\n\n  wire cmp_grp_signed = g_cmp & ~operator_i[0];\n  wire minmax_signed  = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MAX);\n  wire signed_family  = cmp_grp_signed | minmax_signed | g_abs | g_clip;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    if (signed_family) begin\n      if (mode8)       cmp_signed = 4'b1111;\n      else if (mode16) cmp_signed = 4'b1010;\n      else             cmp_signed = 4'b1000;\n    end else cmp_signed = 4'b0000;\n  end\n\n  genvar ci;\n  generate\n    for (ci = 0; ci < 4; ci++) begin : gen_cmp_vec\n      assign is_equal_vec[ci] = (operand_a_i[8*ci+7:8*ci] == operand_b_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]);\n      assign is_greater_vec[ci] = $signed({operand_a_i[8*ci+7] & cmp_signed[ci], operand_a_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]})\n                                > $signed({operand_b_i[8*ci+7] & cmp_signed[ci], operand_b_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]});\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic [3:0] is_equal, is_greater;\n  always_comb begin\n    is_equal   = {4{is_equal_vec[3] & is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[1] & is_equal_vec[0]}};\n    is_greater = {4{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & (is_greater_vec[2]\n                                       | (is_equal_vec[2] & (is_greater_vec[1]\n                                       | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])))))}};\n    if (mode16) begin\n      is_equal[1:0]   = {2{is_equal_vec[0] & is_equal_vec[1]}};\n      is_equal[3:2]   = {2{is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[3]}};\n      is_greater[1:0] = {2{is_greater_vec[1] | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])}};\n      is_greater[3:2] = {2{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & is_greater_vec[2])}};\n    end else if (mode8) begin\n      is_equal   = is_equal_vec;\n      is_greater = is_greater_vec;\n    end\n  end\n\n  logic [3:0] cmp_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    unique case (operator_i[6:2])\n      5'b00000: cmp_result = ~(is_greater | is_equal);\n      5'b00001: cmp_result = ~is_greater;\n      5'b00010: cmp_result = operator_i[1] ? (is_greater | is_equal) : is_greater;\n      5'b00011: cmp_result = operator_i[0] ? ~is_equal : is_equal;\n      default:  cmp_result = is_equal;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n\n  wire do_min = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MINU) || g_clip;\n  wire [3:0] sel_minmax = is_greater ^ {4{do_min}};\n  wire [31:0] minmax_b = g_abs ? adder_result : operand_b_i;\n  wire [31:0] result_minmax = {sel_minmax[3] ? operand_a_i[31:24] : minmax_b[31:24],\n                               sel_minmax[2] ? operand_a_i[23:16] : minmax_b[23:16],\n                               sel_minmax[1] ? operand_a_i[15:8]  : minmax_b[15:8],\n                               sel_minmax[0] ? operand_a_i[7:0]   : minmax_b[7:0]};\n\n  wire [31:0] operand_b_neg = ~operand_b_i;\n  wire is_equal_clip = (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) ? (operand_a_i == 32'h0)\n                                                 : (operand_a_i == operand_b_neg);\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    clip_result = result_minmax;\n    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) begin\n      if (operand_a_i[31] || is_equal_clip) clip_result = '0;\n    end else begin\n      if (adder_result_expanded[36] || is_equal_clip) clip_result = operand_b_neg;\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // shuffle / pack / ext / ins\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] shuf_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    shuf_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_PCKLO: shuf_result = mode8 ? {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0]}\n                                      : {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[15:0]};\n      ALU_PCKHI: shuf_result = mode8 ? {operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0], operand_c_i[15:0]}\n                                      : {operand_a_i[31:16], operand_b_i[31:16]};\n      ALU_SHUF: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result = {operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8]   +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0]   +: 8]};\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result = {operand_b_i[16] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0],\n                         operand_b_i[0]  ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_SHUF2: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result[31:24] = operand_b_i[26] ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[23:16] = operand_b_i[18] ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[15:8]  = operand_b_i[10] ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[7:0]   = operand_b_i[2]  ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0] +: 8];\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result[31:16] = operand_b_i[17] ? (operand_b_i[16] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0])\n                                               : (operand_b_i[16] ? operand_c_i[31:16] : operand_c_i[15:0]);\n          shuf_result[15:0]  = operand_b_i[1]  ?
