(operand_a_i[31] ? 6'd31 : 6'd0) : clb_result;\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // divider (cycle-exact FSM; comparator from subtractor)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  wire         div_valid = enable_i & g_div;\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (shift_left_result),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(~|operand_a_i),\n      .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO(result_div),\n      .InVld_SI (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)\n  );\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // result mux (grouped OR)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] logic_result = (operator_i == ALU_AND) ? (operand_a_i | operand_b_i) :\n                                                       (operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i);\n\n  wire [31:0] cmp_fill = {{8{cmp_result[3]}}, {8{cmp_result[2]}}, {8{cmp_result[1]}}, {8{cmp_result[0]}}};\n  wire scalar_setless = g_cmp & operator_i[1] & ~operator_i[3];\n\n  wire [31:0] minmax_result = g_abs ? (is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]}\n                                                  : result_minmax)\n                                    : (g_clip ? clip_result : result_minmax);\n\n  wire g_rs_b_use = (g_shift & ~sll_op);   // SRA SRL ROR -> shift_result\n\n  assign result_o = ({32{g_logic}}   & logic_result)\n                  | ({32{g_addfam}}  & rs_a_result)\n                  | ({32{g_rs_b_use}} & shift_result)\n                  | ({32{sll_op}}    & shift_left_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bmanip}}  & bmanip_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bitcnt}}  & {26'h0, bitop_result})\n                  | ({32{g_extins | g_shuf}} & shuf_result)\n                  | ({32{g_cmp & ~scalar_setless}} & cmp_fill)\n                  | ({32{scalar_setless}} & {31'b0, cmp_result[3]})\n                  | ({32{g_minmax | g_abs | g_clip}} & minmax_result)\n                  | ({32{g_div}}     & result_div);\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- popcount ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n    for (n = 0; OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? bj < 32; bj++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[bj] = shift_result[31-bj];\n    end\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 16; bj++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*bj+1:2*bj] = shift_result[31-bj*2:31-bj*2-1];\n    end\n    for (bj = 0; bj < 10; bj++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*bj+2:3*bj] = shift_result[31-bj*3:31-bj*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n  always_comb begin\n    unique case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n      2'b01:   brev_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10:   brev_result = radix_8_rev;\n      default: brev_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  logic [31:0] bmanip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    if (operator_i == ALU_BCLR)       bmanip_result = bclr_result;\n    else if (operator_i == ALU_BSET)  bmanip_result = bset_result;\n    else if (g_brev)                  bmanip_result = brev_result;\n    else                              bmanip_result = bextins_result;\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // comparisons + min/max/abs/clip\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [3:0] cmp_signed;\n  wire [3:0] is_equal_vec;\n  wire [3:0] is_greater_vec;\n\n  wire cmp_grp_signed = g_cmp & ~operator_i[0];\n  wire minmax_signed  = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MAX);\n  wire signed_family  = cmp_grp_signed | minmax_signed | g_abs | g_clip;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    if (signed_family) begin\n      if (mode8)       cmp_signed = 4'b1111;\n      else if (mode16) cmp_signed = 4'b1010;\n      else             cmp_signed = 4'b1000;\n    end else cmp_signed = 4'b0000;\n  end\n\n  genvar ci;\n  generate\n    for (ci = 0; ci < 4; ci++) begin : gen_cmp_vec\n      assign is_equal_vec[ci] = (operand_a_i[8*ci+7:8*ci] == operand_b_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]);\n      assign is_greater_vec[ci] = $signed({operand_a_i[8*ci+7] & cmp_signed[ci], operand_a_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]})\n                                > $signed({operand_b_i[8*ci+7] & cmp_signed[ci], operand_b_i[8*ci+7:8*ci]});\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic [3:0] is_equal, is_greater;\n  always_comb begin\n    is_equal   = {4{is_equal_vec[3] & is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[1] & is_equal_vec[0]}};\n    is_greater = {4{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & (is_greater_vec[2]\n                                       | (is_equal_vec[2] & (is_greater_vec[1]\n                                       | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])))))}};\n    if (mode16) begin\n      is_equal[1:0]   = {2{is_equal_vec[0] & is_equal_vec[1]}};\n      is_equal[3:2]   = {2{is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[3]}};\n      is_greater[1:0] = {2{is_greater_vec[1] | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])}};\n      is_greater[3:2] = {2{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & is_greater_vec[2])}};\n    end else if (mode8) begin\n      is_equal   = is_equal_vec;\n      is_greater = is_greater_vec;\n    end\n  end\n\n  logic [3:0] cmp_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    unique case (operator_i[6:2])\n      5'b00000: cmp_result = ~(is_greater | is_equal);\n      5'b00001: cmp_result = ~is_greater;\n      5'b00010: cmp_result = operator_i[1] ? (is_greater | is_equal) : is_greater;\n      5'b00011: cmp_result = operator_i[0] ? ~is_equal : is_equal;\n      default:  cmp_result = is_equal;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n\n  wire do_min = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MINU) || g_clip;\n  wire [3:0] sel_minmax = is_greater ^ {4{do_min}};\n  wire [31:0] minmax_b = g_abs ? adder_result : operand_b_i;\n  wire [31:0] result_minmax = {sel_minmax[3] ? operand_a_i[31:24] : minmax_b[31:24],\n                               sel_minmax[2] ? operand_a_i[23:16] : minmax_b[23:16],\n                               sel_minmax[1] ? operand_a_i[15:8]  : minmax_b[15:8],\n                               sel_minmax[0] ? operand_a_i[7:0]   : minmax_b[7:0]};\n\n  wire [31:0] operand_b_neg = ~operand_b_i;\n  wire is_equal_clip = (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) ? (operand_a_i == 32'h0)\n                                                 : (operand_a_i == operand_b_neg);\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    clip_result = result_minmax;\n    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) begin\n      if (operand_a_i[31] || is_equal_clip) clip_result = '0;\n    end else begin\n      if (adder_result_expanded[36] || is_equal_clip) clip_result = operand_b_neg;\n    end\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // shuffle / pack / ext / ins\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] shuf_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    shuf_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_PCKLO: shuf_result = mode8 ? {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0]}\n                                      : {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[15:0]};\n      ALU_PCKHI: shuf_result = mode8 ? {operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0], operand_c_i[15:0]}\n                                      : {operand_a_i[31:16], operand_b_i[31:16]};\n      ALU_SHUF: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result = {operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8]   +: 8],\n                         operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0]   +: 8]};\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result = {operand_b_i[16] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0],\n                         operand_b_i[0]  ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_SHUF2: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result[31:24] = operand_b_i[26] ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[25:24] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[23:16] = operand_b_i[18] ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[17:16] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[15:8]  = operand_b_i[10] ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[9:8] +: 8];\n          shuf_result[7:0]   = operand_b_i[2]  ? operand_a_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0] +: 8]\n                                               : operand_c_i[8*operand_b_i[1:0] +: 8];\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result[31:16] = operand_b_i[17] ? (operand_b_i[16] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0])\n                                               : (operand_b_i[16] ? operand_c_i[31:16] : operand_c_i[15:0]);\n          shuf_result[15:0]  = operand_b_i[1]  ? (operand_b_i[0] ? operand_a_i[31:16] : operand_a_i[15:0])\n                                               : (operand_b_i[0] ? operand_c_i[31:16] : operand_c_i[15:0]);\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_EXT: begin\n        if (mode8)      shuf_result = {24'h0, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8]};\n        else            shuf_result = {16'h0, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n      end\n      ALU_EXTS: begin\n        if (mode8)      shuf_result = {{24{operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i + 7]}}, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8]};\n        else            shuf_result = {{16{operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] + 15]}}, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n      end\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        if (mode8) begin\n          shuf_result = operand_c_i;\n          shuf_result[8*imm_vec_ext_i +: 8] = operand_a_i[7:0];\n        end else begin\n          shuf_result = imm_vec_ext_i[0] ? {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_c_i[15:0]}\n                                         : {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // bit counting\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [5:0] cnt_result;\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (.in_i(operand_a_i), .result_o(cnt_result));\n\n  wire [4:0] fl1_result = 5'd31 - ff1_result;\n\n  logic [5:0] bitop_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    bitop_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};\n      ALU_FL1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_result};\n      ALU_CNT: bitop_result = cnt_result;\n      ALU_CLB: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? (operand_a_i[31] ? 6'd31 : 6'd0) : clb_result;\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // divider (cycle-exact FSM; loop-optimized datapath)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  wire         div_valid = enable_i & g_div;\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (shift_left_result),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(~|operand_a_i),\n      .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO(result_div),\n      .InVld_SI (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)\n  );\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  // result mux (grouped OR)\n  // ----------------------------------------------------------------\n  wire [31:0] logic_result = (operator_i == ALU_AND) ? (operand_a_i & operand_b_i) :\n                             (operator_i == ALU_OR)  ? (operand_a_i | operand_b_i) :\n                                                       (operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i);\n\n  wire [31:0] cmp_fill = {{8{cmp_result[3]}}, {8{cmp_result[2]}}, {8{cmp_result[1]}}, {8{cmp_result[0]}}};\n  wire scalar_setless = g_cmp & operator_i[1] & ~operator_i[3];\n\n  wire [31:0] minmax_result = g_abs ? (is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]}\n                                                  : result_minmax)\n                                    : (g_clip ? clip_result : result_minmax);\n\n  wire g_rs_b_use = (g_shift & ~sll_op);   // SRA SRL ROR\n\n  assign result_o = ({32{g_logic}}   & logic_result)\n                  | ({32{g_addfam}}  & rs_a_result)\n                  | ({32{g_rs_b_use}} & shift_result)\n                  | ({32{sll_op}}    & shift_left_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bmanip}}  & bmanip_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bitcnt}}  & {26'h0, bitop_result})\n                  | ({32{g_extins | g_shuf}} & shuf_result)\n                  | ({32{g_cmp & ~scalar_setless}} & cmp_fill)\n                  | ({32{scalar_setless}} & {31'b0, cmp_result[3]})\n                  | ({32{g_minmax | g_abs | g_clip}} & minmax_result)\n                  | ({32{g_div}}     & result_div);\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- popcount ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- Kogge-Stone subtractor: {ge, diff} = a - b\nmodule ks_subn #(\n    parameter int N = 32\n) (\n    input  logic [N-1:0] a,\n    input  logic [N-1:0] b,\n    output logic         ge,     // unsigned a >= b (carry out)\n    output logic [N-1:0] diff\n);\n  localparam int L = $clog2(N);\n  wire [N-1:0] Ax = a;\n  wire [N-1:0] Bx = ~b;   // a + ~b + 1\n\n  logic [L:0][N-1:0] G, P;\n\n  assign G[0] = Ax & Bx;\n  assign P[0] = Ax ^ Bx;\n\n  genvar lvl, i;\n  generate\n    for (lvl = 1; lvl <= L; lvl++) begin : gen_lvl\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : gen_bit\n        if (i >= (1 << (lvl-1))) begin : gen_comb\n          assign G[lvl][i] = G[lvl-1][i] | (P[lvl-1][i] & G[lvl-1][i - (1<<(lvl-1))]);\n          assign P[lvl][i] = P[lvl-1][i] & P[lvl-1][i - (1<<(lvl-1))];\n        end else begin : gen_pass\n          assign G[lvl][i] = G[lvl-1][i];\n          assign P[lvl][i] = P[lvl-1][i];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // cin = 1 folded into bit 0: carry_in[0] = 1; carry_in[i>0] = G[i-1] | P[i-1]\n  assign diff = P[0] ^ {(G[L][N-2:0] | P[L][N-2:0]), 1'b1};\n  assign ge = G[L][N-1] | P[L][N-1];\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------- serial divider: cycle-exact FSM, loop-optimized datapath ----------------\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  localparam int W = C_WIDTH;\n\n  logic [W-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [W-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [W-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [W-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [W-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [W-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  } State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[W-1] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, BReg_DP[W-1:1]};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < W; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[W-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n\n  // ---- loop subtractor: AReg - BReg directly from registers (no FSM decode)\n  wire        loop_ge;\n  wire [W-1:0] loop_diff;\n  ks_subn #(.N(W)) ks_loop (.a(AReg_DP), .b(BReg_DP), .ge(loop_ge), .diff(loop_diff));\n\n  wire loop_eq = ~(|loop_diff);\n  // upstream comparator: (A==B) | ((A>B) ^ CompInv); A>B = ge & ~eq\n  wire ab_cmp_raw = CompInv_SP ? (~loop_ge | loop_eq) : loop_ge;\n  assign ABComp_S = ab_cmp_raw & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n\n  // ---- load path: +/- OpA (only used in the load cycle)\n  wire [W-1:0] neg_opa = ~OpA_DI + {{(W-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1};\n  wire [W-1:0] load_a  = PmSel_S ? OpA_DI : neg_opa;\n\n  assign AReg_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? load_a : (ABComp_S ? loop_diff : AReg_DP);\n\n  assign Cnt_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBShift_DI : (~CntZero_S) ? Cnt_DP - 1 : Cnt_DP;\n  assign CntZero_S = ~(|Cnt_DP);\n\n  always_comb begin : p_fsm\n    State_SN   = State_SP;\n    OutVld_SO  = 1'b0;\n    LoadEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ARegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    BRegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ResRegEn_S = 1'b0;\n\n    case (State_SP)\n      IDLE: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (InVld_SI) begin\n          OutVld_SO = 1'b0;\n          ARegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          BRegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          LoadEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          State_SN  = DIVIDE;\n        end\n      end\n      DIVIDE: begin\n        ARegEn_S   = ABComp_S;\n        BRegEn_S   = 1'b1;\n        ResRegEn_S = 1'b1;\n        if (CntZero_S) begin\n          State_SN = FINISH;\n        end\n      end\n      FINISH: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (OutRdy_SI) begin\n          State_SN = IDLE;\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign RemSel_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpCode_SI[1] : RemSel_SP;\n  assign CompInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBSign_SI : CompInv_SP;\n  assign ResInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? (~OpBIsZero_SI | OpCode_SI[1]) & OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[W-1] ^ OpBSign_SI) : ResInv_SP;\n\n  assign BReg_DN = (BRegEn_S) ? BMux_D : BReg_DP;\n  assign ResReg_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? ## Summary

**Best design: `design8.sv` — 634,178 ADP** (area 519, delay 1221 ps), beating the baseline copy `design.sv` (645,629; area 441, delay 1462 ps).

### What worked
1. **De-sharing the datapath** (the main lever): the upstream's critical path was `operator_i` → opcode mux → shared 64-bit shifter → result mux. I gave the adder family its own dedicated per-lane right shifter (`rs_a`, fed by the round-sum) and kept one upstream-style shared barrel (right-shift of bit-reversed data = left shift) for SRA/SRL/ROR/BEXT/BEXTU/SLL/BINS/BREV/div. This cut delay from 1462→1221 ps.
2. **Cheaper divider comparator**: derived `(A==B)|((A>B)^CompInv)` from the subtractor's carry-out and zero-flag instead of two 32-bit compare trees (~15 area units saved).
3. **One-hot FF1** (`x & -x` + priority encode) replacing the upstream's 31-node mux tree; direct `~|operand_a` for divide-by-zero.
4. **Grouped OR result mux** instead of a deep case-based mux.
5. **36-bit segmented adder** kept, with separator bits expressed as simple functions of the negate-type op flag and vector mode.

### What failed / regressed
- **`>>>` context bug (twice!)**: `$signed(x) >>> n` inside a conditional/mixed-width expression gets evaluated in unsigned context and becomes a logical shift. Fix: compute arith shifts as standalone continuous assigns.
- **design5 (906k)**: materializing both arith and logical barrel outputs everywhere doubled area.
- **design9 (658k)**: Kogge-Stone subtractor in the divider added area for no net win over the plain subtractor + carry trick.
- **design11 (713k)**: loop-optimized divider (KS sub fed straight from registers, separate load adder) fixed the FF→FF path but moved the critical path back to the combinational `operator_i`→`result_o` chain.
- `target_delay` experiments: 2000 ps raised ADP to 715k; 100 ps changed nothing. Default 500 ps is best.
- Numerous functional bugs (adder separator placement, ROR `{a,a}` width, dropped result-mux terms, SHUF2 polarity) — all caught by the equivalence TB.

### Lessons for next time
- In this metric, **delay dominates ADP**; area savings only matter if they don't lengthen the critical path. The winning move is getting opcode decode *off* every datapath path.
- Verify shift expressions under `always_comb` conditionals immediately — the `>>>` unsigned-context trap is silent and cost two eval cycles each occurrence.
- When a loop (divider) is fixed-latency by contract, its internal microarchitecture is free to change as long as register timing matches — but combinational restructuring can backfire by shifting the global critical path; re-check the worst path after each change.
- Start from the known-correct baseline, change one family at a time, and evaluate after every single edit.
